 Java Unit Tests:

```java
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class DepthTrackingJsonGeneratorTest {
    private ObjectMapper mapper = new ObjectMapper();

    @Test
    public void testGetCurrentDepth() throws Exception {
        JsonGenerator gen = mapper.getPolymorphicSerializer(String.class).createGenerator(null);
        DepthTrackingJsonGenerator trackingGen = new DepthTrackingJsonGenerator(gen);

        trackingGen.writeStartObject(); // depth = 1
        JsonNode node = mapper.readTree(trackingGen);
        assertEquals(0, trackingGen.getCurrentDepth());

        trackingGen.writeFieldName("field");
        node = mapper.readTree(trackingGen);
        assertEquals(0, trackingGen.getCurrentDepth());

        trackingGen.writeStartObject(); // depth = 2 (nested object)
        node = mapper.readTree(trackingGen);
        assertEquals(1, trackingGen.getCurrentDepth());

        trackingGen.writeEndObject(); // depth = 1
        node = mapper.readTree(trackingGen);
        assertEquals(0, trackingGen.getCurrentDepth());

        trackingGen.writeFieldName("field");
        node = mapper.readTree(trackingGen);
        assertEquals(0, trackingGen.getCurrentDepth());
    }
}
```

 Java Unit Tests:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import weka.core.Instances;
import weka.core.Instance;

public class SchemeCheckTest {
    @Test
    public void testCheckIfSchemeAltersTrainingData() {
        Instances before = createSampleDataSet();
        Instances after = deepCopy(before);

        // Modify a single instance in the 'after' dataset to simulate alteration
        after.instance(0).setValue(0, "ModifiedValue");

        boolean result = SchemeCheck.checkIfSchemeAltersTrainingData(before, after);
        assertTrue(result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testUnchangedTrainingData() {
        Instances before = createSampleDataSet();
        Instances after = deepCopy(before);

        boolean result = SchemeCheck.checkIfSchemeAltersTrainingData(before, after);
        assertFalse(result);
    }

    private Instances deepCopy(Instances instances) {
        try {
            return instances.copyStructure(instances);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Failed to copy the data", e);
        }
    }

    private Instances createSampleDataSet() {
        // Initialize a sample dataset with 3 attributes and 5 instances
        Instances result = new Instances(new String[]{"A1", "A2", "A3"}, 0);
        result.setClassIndex(result.numAttributes() - 1);
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            try {
                Instance instance = new DenseInstance(result.numAttributes());
                instance.setValue(0, "Value1");
                instance.setValue(1, i % 2 == 0 ? 1.0 : 2.0);
                instance.setValue(2, i);
                result.add(instance);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                throw new RuntimeException("Failed to create a sample dataset", e);
            }
        }
        return result;
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests:

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class ProgramTests
{
    [Test]
    public void TestDatasetIntegrity()
    {
        var before = CreateSampleDataSet();
        var after = DeepCopy(before);

        // Modify a single instance in the 'after' dataset to simulate alteration
        after[0].Values[0] = "ModifiedValue";

        bool[] result = Program.DatasetIntegrity(
            nominalPredictor: true,
            numericPredictor: true,
            stringPredictor: false,
            datePredictor: false,
            relationalPredictor: false,
            multiInstance: false,
            classType: 1,
            predictorMissing: false,
            classMissing: false);

        Assert.IsTrue(result[0]); // check if header structure changed
        Assert.AreEqual(before.NumInstances, after.NumInstances); // check if number of instances unchanged
        Assert.IsFalse(result[1]); // check if instance order unchanged (would be true if the dataset is small)
        Assert.AreEqual(before[0].Values[0], after[0].Values[0]); // check if instance weights unchanged
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestUnchangedTrainingData()
    {
        var before = CreateSampleDataSet();
        var after = DeepCopy(before);

        bool[] result = Program.DatasetIntegrity(
            nominalPredictor: true,
            numericPredictor: true,
            stringPredictor: false,
            datePredictor: false,
            relationalPredictor: false,
            multiInstance: false,
            classType: 1,
            predictorMissing: false,
            classMissing: false);

        Assert.IsFalse(result[0]); // check if header structure unchanged
        Assert.AreEqual(before.NumInstances, after.NumInstances); // check if number of instances unchanged
        Assert.IsTrue(result[1]); // check if instance order unchanged (would be false if the dataset is small)
        Assert.AreEqual(before[0].Values[0], after[0].Values[0]); // check if instance weights unchanged
    }

    private Instances CreateSampleDataSet()
    {
        var attributes = new List<string> { "A1", "A2", "A3" };
        int numInstances = 5;
        Instances result = new Instances(attributes, numInstances);
        result.SetClassIndex(result.NumAttributes() - 1);

        for (int i = 0; i < numInstances; i++)
        {
            var instance = new DenseInstance(result.NumAttributes());
            instance.Value(0) = "Value1";
            instance.Value[1] = i % 2 == 0 ? 1.0 : 2.0;
            instance.Value[2] = i;
            result.Add(instance);
        }

        return result;
    }

    private Instances DeepCopy(Instances instances)
    {
        var result = new Instances(instances);
        result.SetAttributeValues(0, instances.Attribute(0).Values);
        for (int i = 1; i < instances.NumAttributes(); i++)
            result.AddAttribute(instances.Attribute(i));

        int[] classValue = instances.ClassValue();
        if (classValue != null && classValue.Length > 0)
            result.SetClassIndex(result.NumAttributes() - 1);

        for (int i = 0; i < instances.NumInstances(); i++)
        {
            var instance = new DenseInstance(result.NumAttributes());
            foreach (var value in instances[i].AttributeValues())
                instance.Value[value.Index] = value.Value;
            result.Add(instance);
        }

        return result;
    }
}
```

```java
import java.util.BitSet;
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class BitSetUnionSizeTest {
    @Test
    public void testBitSetUnionSize() {
        BitSet set1 = new BitSet();
        BitSet set2 = new BitSet();

        set1.set(1);
        set1.set(3);
        set1.set(5);

        set2.set(2);
        set2.set(3);
        set2.set(6);

        BitSet union = (BitSet) set1.clone();
        union.or(set2);
        int unionSize = union.cardinality();
        assertEquals(4, unionSize);
    }

    @Test
    public void testEmptyUnion() {
        BitSet set1 = new BitSet();
        BitSet set2 = new BitSet();
        BitSet union = (BitSet) set1.clone();
        union.or(set2);
        int unionSize = union.cardinality();
        assertEquals(0, unionSize);
    }

    @Test
    public void testSingleBitUnion() {
        BitSet set1 = new BitSet();
        set1.set(1);
        BitSet set2 = new BitSet();
        set2.set(2);
        BitSet union = (BitSet) set1.clone();
        union.or(set2);
        int unionSize = union.cardinality();
        assertEquals(1, unionSize);
    }

    @Test
    public void testIdenticalUnion() {
        BitSet set1 = new BitSet();
        BitSet set2 = new BitSet();

        set1.set(1);
        set1.set(2);
        set2.set(1);
        set2.set(2);
        BitSet union = (BitSet) set1.clone();
        union.or(set2);
        int unionSize = union.cardinality();
        assertEquals(2, unionSize);
    }

    @Test
    public void testOppositeUnion() {
        BitSet set1 = new BitSet();
        BitSet set2 = new BitSet();

        set1.set(1);
        set1.set(2);
        set2.set(0);
        set2.flip(0);
        BitSet union = (BitSet) set1.clone();
        union.or(set2);
        int unionSize = union.cardinality();
        assertEquals(3, unionSize);
    }
}
```

 Java Unit Tests:

```java
import java.util.*;

public class TestOrder {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        TestOrder test = new TestOrder();
        test.test1();
        test.test2();
        test.test3();
        test.test4();
        test.test5();
    }

    private Map<String, String> local = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private Map<String, String> remote = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    public TestOrder() {
        local.put("key1", "val1");
        local.put("key2", "localVal2");
        local.put("key3", "val3");

        remote.put("key2", "remoteVal2");
    }

    public void test1() {
        Map<String, String> merged = TestOrder.mergeMaps(local, null);
        assertEquals("val1", merged.get("key1"));
        assertEquals("localVal2", merged.get("key2"));
        assertEquals("val3", merged.get("key3"));
    }

    public void test2() {
        Map<String, String> merged = TestOrder.mergeMaps(remote, null);
        assertEquals("null", merged.get("key1"));
        assertEquals("remoteVal2", merged.get("key2"));
        assertEquals("null", merged.get("key3"));
    }

    public void test3() {
        Map<String, String> merged = TestOrder.mergeMaps(local, remote);
        assertEquals("val1", merged.get("key1"));
        assertEquals("remoteVal2", merged.get("key2"));
        assertEquals("val3", merged.get("key3"));
    }

    public void test4() {
        Map<String, String> merged = TestOrder.mergeMaps(null, local);
        assertEquals("val1", merged.get("key1"));
        assertEquals("localVal2", merged.get("key2"));
        assertEquals("val3", merged.get("key3"));
    }

    public void test5() {
        Map<String, String> merged = TestOrder.mergeMaps(remote, local);
        assertEquals("null", merged.get("key1"));
        assertEquals("remoteVal2", merged.get("key2"));
        assertEquals("val3", merged.get("key3"));
    }

    public static Map<String, String> mergeMaps(Map<String, String> local, Map<String, String> remote) {
        if (local == null && remote == null) return null;

        Map<String, String> merged = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        if (local != null) {
            for (String key : local.keySet()) {
                if (remote != null && remote.containsKey(key)) {
                    merged.put(key, remote.get(key));
                } else {
                    merged.put(key, local.get(key));
                }
            }
        }
        if (remote != null) {
            for (String key : remote.keySet()) {
                if (!merged.containsKey(key)) {
                    merged.put(key, remote.get(key));
                }
            }
        }
        return merged;
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests:

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;

public class TestOrder
{
    private Map<string, string> local = new LinkedHashMap<string, string>();
    private Map<string, string> remote = new LinkedHashMap<string, string>();

    [SetUp]
    public void Setup()
    {
        local = new LinkedHashMap<string, string>();
        remote = new LinkedHashMap<string, string>();

        local.Add("key1", "val1");
        local.Add("key2", "localVal2");
        local.Add("key3", "val3");

        remote.Add("key2", "remoteVal2");
    }

    [Test]
    public void test1()
    {
        var merged = TestOrder.MergeMaps(local, null);
        Assert.AreEqual("val1", merged["key1"]);
        Assert.AreEqual("localVal2", merged["key2"]);
        Assert.AreEqual("val3", merged["key3"]);
    }

    [Test]
    public void test2()
    {
        var merged = TestOrder.MergeMaps(remote, null);
        Assert.AreEqual(null, merged["key1"]);
        Assert.AreEqual("remoteVal2", merged["key2"]);
        Assert.AreEqual(null, merged["key3"]);
    }

    [Test]
    public void test3()
    {
        var merged = TestOrder.MergeMaps(local, remote);
        Assert.AreEqual("val1", merged["key1"]);
        Assert.AreEqual("remoteVal2", merged["key2"]);
        Assert.AreEqual("val3", merged["key3"]);
    }

    [Test]
    public void test4()
    {
        var merged = TestOrder.MergeMaps(null, local);
        Assert.AreEqual("val1", merged["key1"]);
        Assert.AreEqual("localVal2", merged["key2"]);
        Assert.AreEqual("val3", merged["key3"]);
    }

    [Test]
    public void test5()
    {
        var merged = TestOrder.MergeMaps(remote, local);
        Assert.AreEqual(null, merged["key1"]);
        Assert.AreEqual("remoteVal2", merged["key2"]);
        Assert.AreEqual("val3", merged["key3"]);
    }

    public static LinkedHashMap<string, string> MergeMaps(LinkedHashMap<string, string> local, LinkedHashMap<string, string> remote)
    {
        if (local == null && remote == null) return null;

        var merged = new LinkedHashMap<string, string>();
        if (local != null)
        {
            foreach (var key in local.Keys)
            {
                if (remote != null && remote.ContainsKey(key))
                {
                    merged[key] = remote[key];
                }
                else
                {
                    merged[key] = local[key];
                }
            }
        }
        if (remote != null)
        {
            foreach (var key in remote.Keys)
            {
                if (!merged.ContainsKey(key))
                {
                    merged[key] = remote[key];
                }
            }
        }
        return merged;
    }
}
```

```java
public class DialogHandlerTest {

    private DialogHandler dialogHandler;

    @Before
    public void setup() {
        dialogHandler = new DialogHandler();
    }

    @Test
    public void testOnDialogOkClosed_DefaultState() {
        dialogHandler.onDialogOkClosed();
        assertEquals("OK button clicked. Performing close actions...", System.out.getMessage());
    }

    @Test
    public void testOnDialogOkClosed_CustomMessage() {
        System.out.println("Custom message");
        dialogHandler = new DialogHandler() {
            @Override
            public void onDialogOkClosed() {
                super.onDialogOkClosed();
                System.out.println(System.out.getMessage());
            }
        };
        dialogHandler.onDialogOkClosed();
        assertEquals("OK button clicked.\nCustom message", System.out.getOut().toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testOnDialogOkClosed_ThrowException() {
        Throwable exception = assertThrows(RuntimeException.class, () -> dialogHandler.onDialogOkClosed());
        assertEquals("Performing close actions...", exception.getMessage());
    }

    @Test
    public void testOnDialogOkClosed_NullObject() {
        DialogHandler nullDialogHandler = null;
        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -> nullDialogHandler.onDialogOkClosed());
    }

    @Test
    public void testOnDialogOkClosed_EmptyClass() {
        class EmptyDialogHandler extends DialogHandler {
            // No implementation for onDialogOkClosed method
        }
        EmptyDialogHandler emptyDialogHandler = new EmptyDialogHandler();
        assertDoesNotThrow(() -> emptyDialogHandler.onDialogOkClosed());
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class SectorTest {
    @Test
    public void testUnion() {
        Sector sector1 = new Sector(-20.0, 10.0, 30.0, 70.0);
        Sector sector2 = new Sector(0.0, 40.0, 20.0, 50.0);
        Sector unionSector = sector1.union(sector2);
        assertEquals("Sector[minLatitude=-20.00, maxLatitude=40.00, minLongitude=20.00, maxLongitude=70.00]", unionSector.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testUnionWithSameSectors() {
        Sector sector1 = new Sector(-20.0, 40.0, 30.0, 70.0);
        Sector unionSector = sector1.union(sector1);
        assertEquals("Sector[minLatitude=-20.00, maxLatitude=40.00, minLongitude=30.00, maxLongitude=70.00]", unionSector.toString());
    }

    @Test(expected = IllegalArgumentException.class)
    public void testUnionWithInvalidSectors() {
        Sector sector1 = new Sector(-91.0, 91.0, -181.0, 181.0);
        Sector sector2 = new Sector(91.0, -91.0, 181.0, -181.0);
        sector1.union(sector2);
    }

    @Test
    public void testUnionWithEmptySectors() {
        Sector emptySector = new Sector(90.0, -90.0, 180.0, -180.0);
        Sector unionSector = emptySector.union(emptySector);
        assertEquals("Sector[minLatitude=90.00, maxLatitude=-90.00, minLongitude=180.00, maxLongitude=-180.00]", unionSector.toString());
    }
}
```

```java
public class DoubleEqualityCheckerTest {
    private static final double EPSILON = 1e-9;

    @org.junit.jupiter.api.Test
    public void testDoubleEquality() {
        DoubleEqualityChecker checker = new DoubleEqualityChecker();
        assertTrue(checker.isEqual(0.3, 0.3 + EPSILON));
        assertFalse(checker.isEqual(0.1 + 0.2, 0.3 - EPSILON));
        assertFalse(checker.isEqual(0.1 + 0.2, 0.15));
        assertFalse(checker.isEqual(0.1 + 0.2, 0.4));
        assertFalse(checker.isEqual(-0.1 - 0.2, 0.3));
    }

    public boolean isEqual(double a, double b) {
        return Math.abs(a - b) < EPSILON;
    }
}
```

Java:

```java
public class DoubleEqualityChecker {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        double a = 0.1 + 0.2;
        double b = 0.3;
        double epsilon = 1e-9;

        if (Math.abs(a - b) < epsilon) {
            System.out.println("a and b are equal (within tolerance).");
        } else {
            System.out.println("a and b are NOT equal.");
        }
}

public class DoubleEqualityCheckerTest {
    private static final double EPSILON = 1e-9;

    @org.junit.jupiter.api.Test
    public void testDoubleEquality() {
        DoubleEqualityChecker checker = new DoubleEqualityChecker();
        Assertions.assertFalse(checker.isEqual(0.1 + 0.2, 0.3)); // a and b are NOT equal.
    }

    public boolean isEqual(double a, double b) {
        return Math.abs(a - b) < EPSILON;
    }
}
```

C#:

```csharp
using System;

class Program
{
    private const double _NUM = 1e-9;

    static void Main(string[] args) {
        double a = 0.1 + 0.2;
        double b = 0.3;

        Console.WriteLine($"Is {a} equal to {b}? {IsDoubleEqual(a, b)}"); // Is 0.3 equal to 0.3? True
    }

    private static bool IsDoubleEqual(double value, double valueToCompare) {
        return (Math.Abs(value - valueToCompare) < _NUM);
    }
}

[TestFixture]
public class DoubleEqualityCheckerTests {
    private const double _NUM = 1e-9;

    [Test]
    public void TestDoubleEquality() {
        var checker = new DoubleEqualityChecker();
        Assert.IsTrue(checker.IsDoubleEqual(0.3, 0.3 + _NUM)); // Is 0.3 equal to 0.31? True
        Assert.IsFalse(checker.IsDoubleEqual(0.1 + 0.2, 0.3 - _NUM)); // Is 0.3 equal to 0.29? False
        Assert.IsFalse(checker.IsDoubleEqual(0.1 + 0.2, 0.15)); // Is 0.3 equal to 0.15? False
        Assert.IsFalse(checker.IsDoubleEqual(0.1 + 0.2, 0.4)); // Is 0.3 equal to 0.4? False
        Assert.IsFalse(checker.IsDoubleEqual(-0.1 - 0.2, 0.3)); // Is -0.3 equal to 0.3? False
    }
}
```

 Java Unit Tests:

```java
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;

public class Main {
    @Test
    public void testIntValuePos() {
        BigDecimal bigDecimalValue = new BigDecimal("12345.67");
        int intValue = bigDecimalValue.intValue();
        int expected = 12345;
        assertEquals(expected, intValue);
    }

    @Test
    public void testIntValueNeg() {
        BigDecimal bigDecimalValue = new BigDecimal("-12345.67");
        int intValue = bigDecimalValue.intValue();
        int expected = -12345;
        assertEquals(expected, intValue);
    }

    @Test
    public void testIntValueZero() {
        BigDecimal bigDecimalValue = new BigDecimal("0.00");
        int intValue = bigDecimalValue.intValue();
        int expected = 0;
        assertEquals(expected, intValue);
    }

    @Test
    public void testIntValueHuge() {
        BigDecimal bigDecimalValue = new BigDecimal("9007199254740991.00"); // Maximum value of long in Java
        int intValue = bigDecimalValue.intValue();
        int expected = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        assertEquals(expected, intValue);
    }

    @Test
    public void testIntValueOverflow() {
        BigDecimal bigDecimalValue = new BigDecimal("9007199254740992.00"); // Overflow value for BigDecimal
        int intValue = bigDecimalValue.intValue();
        int expected = Integer.MIN_VALUE; // Maximum negative value that can be stored in an integer
        assertEquals(expected, intValue);
    }
}
```

```csharp
            int result = _NUM;
            Assert.AreEqual(result, (int)aNumber);
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestIntValueNeg()
        {
            decimal aNumber = decimal.Parse("-" + _STR);
            int result = -_NUM;
            Assert.AreEqual(result, (int)aNumber);
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestIntValueZero()
        {
            decimal aNumber = 0m;
            int result = 0;
            Assert.AreEqual(result, (int)aNumber);
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestIntValueHuge()
        {
            decimal aNumber = 9007199254740991m; // Maximum value of long in C#
            int result = Int32.MaxValue;
            Assert.AreEqual(result, (int)aNumber);
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestIntValueOverflow()
        {
            decimal aNumber = 9007199254740992m; // Overflow value for BigDecimal in C#
            int result = Int32.MinValue;
            Assert.AreEqual(result, (int)aNumber);
        }
```

```java
import java.io.File;
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class DirectoryExaminerTest {
    @Test
    public void testExamineDirectory_EmptyDir() {
        File emptyDir = new File("empty");
        emptyDir.mkdir();
        DirectoryExaminer.examineDirectory(emptyDir);
        assertFalse(emptyDir.listFiles().length > 0);
        emptyDir.delete();
    }

    @Test
    public void testExamineDirectory_File() {
        File file = new File("testFile");
        try {
            file.createNewFile();
            DirectoryExaminer.examineDirectory(new File("."));
            assertTrue(file.exists());
            file.delete();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            fail(e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Test
    public void testExamineDirectory_Directory() {
        File testDir = new File("testDir");
        testDir.mkdir();
        File fileInside = new File(testDir, "testFile");
        try {
            fileInside.createNewFile();
            DirectoryExaminer.examineDirectory(new File("."));
            assertTrue(fileInside.exists());
            testDir.delete();
            fileInside.delete();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            fail(e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Test
    public void testExamineDirectory_NestedDirectory() {
        File testDir1 = new File("testDir1");
        testDir1.mkdir();
        File testDir2 = new File(testDir1, "testDir2");
        testDir2.mkdir();
        File fileInside = new File(testDir2, "testFile");
        try {
            fileInside.createNewFile();
            DirectoryExaminer.examineDirectory(new File("."));
            assertTrue(fileInside.exists());
            testDir1.delete();
            testDir2.delete();
            fileInside.delete();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            fail(e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Test
    public void testExamineDirectory_NonExistentDirectory() {
        File nonExistentDir = new File("nonExistent");
        DirectoryExaminer.examineDirectory(nonExistentDir);
        assertFalse(nonExistentDir.exists());
        nonExistentDir.delete();
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests:

```csharp
using System;
using NUnit.Framework;
using System.IO;
using static DirectoryWalkerApp.DirectoryWalker<string>;

[TestFixture]
public class DirectoryWalkerTests {
    [SetUp]
    public void Setup() {
        if (Directory.Exists("empty")) Directory.Delete("empty", true);
        if (Directory.Exists("testDir")) Directory.Delete("testDir", true);
        if (File.Exists("testFile")) File.Delete("testFile");
        if (File.Exists("nonExistent")) File.Delete("nonExistent");
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestEmptyDirectory() {
        var results = new List<string>();
        var walker = new DirectoryWalker<string>();
        var emptyDir = new DirectoryInfo("empty");
        emptyDir.Create();
        walker.Walk(emptyDir, 0, results);
        Assert.IsEmpty(results);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestFile() {
        FileInfo testFile = new FileInfo("testFile");
        testFile.Create();
        var results = new List<string>();
        var walker = new DirectoryWalker<string>();
        var startDir = new DirectoryInfo(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory());
        walker.Walk(startDir, 0, results);
        Assert.IsTrue(testFile.Exists());
        testFile.Delete();
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestDirectory() {
        DirectoryInfo testDir = new DirectoryInfo("testDir");
        testDir.Create();
        FileInfo fileInside = new FileInfo(Path.Combine(testDir.FullName, "testFile"));
        fileInside.Create();
        var results = new List<string>();
        var walker = new DirectoryWalker<string>();
        var startDir = new DirectoryInfo(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory());
        walker.Walk(startDir, 0, results);
        Assert.IsTrue(fileInside.Exists());
        testDir.Delete(true);
        fileInside.Delete();
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestNestedDirectory() {
        DirectoryInfo testDir1 = new DirectoryInfo("testDir1");
        testDir1.Create();
        DirectoryInfo testDir2 = new DirectoryInfo(Path.Combine(testDir1.FullName, "testDir2"));
        testDir2.Create();
        FileInfo fileInside = new FileInfo(Path.Combine(testDir2.FullName, "testFile"));
        fileInside.Create();
        var results = new List<string>();
        var walker = new DirectoryWalker<string>();
        var startDir = new DirectoryInfo(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory());
        walker.Walk(startDir, 0, results);
        Assert.IsTrue(fileInside.Exists());
        testDir1.Delete(true);
        testDir2.Delete(true);
        fileInside.Delete();
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestNonExistentDirectory() {
        var results = new List<string>();
        var walker = new DirectoryWalker<string>();
        var nonExistentDir = new DirectoryInfo("nonExistent");
        walker.Walk(nonExistentDir, 0, results);
        Assert.IsFalse(File.Exists("nonExistent"));
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.*;

public class SortedFieldValuesTest {
    @Test
    public void testGetSortedFieldValues_IntegerField() {
        List<Tuple> tuples = Arrays.asList(
            new Tuple(new Object[] {1, "Alice", 4.5}),
            new Tuple(new Object[] {3, "Bob", 2.1}),
            new Tuple(new Object[] {2, "Carol", 7.8}),
            new Tuple(new Object[] {4, "Dave", 5.3})
        );

        Comparable[] sortedField0 = SortedFieldValues.getSortedFieldValues(new TupleIterator(tuples), 0);
        Collections.sort(Arrays.asList(new Integer[]{1, 2, 3, 4})); // sort the expected result for easy comparison
        assertArrayEquals(new Comparable[]{1, 2, 3, 4}, sortedField0);
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetSortedFieldValues_DoubleField() {
        List<Tuple> tuples = Arrays.asList(
            new Tuple(new Object[] {1, "Alice", 4.5}),
            new Tuple(new Object[] {3, "Bob", 2.1}),
            new Tuple(new Object[] {2, "Carol", 7.8}),
            new Tuple(new Object[] {4, "Dave", 5.3})
        );

        Comparable[] sortedField2 = SortedFieldValues.getSortedFieldValues(new TupleIterator(tuples), 2);
        Collections.sort(Arrays.asList(new Double[]{2.1, 4.5, 7.8, 5.3})); // sort the expected result for easy comparison
        assertArrayEquals(new Comparable[]{2.1, 4.5, 7.8, 5.3}, sortedField2);
    }
}
```

```java
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class MainTest {
    @Test
    public void testDivideInvalidRoundingMode() {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal("10");
        BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal("3");
        try {
            a.divide(b, 2, -1);
            fail("IllegalArgumentException expected");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            assertEquals("Illegal rounding mode: -1", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Test
    public void testDivideValidRoundingMode() {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal("10");
        BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal("3");
        try {
            BigDecimal result = a.divide(b, 2, 0); // RoundingMode.DOWN by default
            assertEquals("3.33", result.toString());
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            fail("No IllegalArgumentException expected");
        }
    }

    @Test(expected = IllegalArgumentException.class)
    public void testDivideNull() {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal("10");
        BigDecimal b = null;
        a.divide(b, 2, 0);
    }

    @Test(expected = ArithmeticException.class)
    public void testDivideByZero() {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal("10");
        BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal("0");
        a.divide(b, 2, 0);
    }

    @Test
    public void testToString() {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal("10.5678");
        assertEquals("10.5678", a.toString());
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using BigDecimalTest;

[TestClass]
public class ProgramTests
{
    [TestMethod]
    public void TestDivideInvalidRoundingMode()
    {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger("10"), 2);
        BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger("3"), 2);
        ExceptionAssert.ThrowsException<ArgumentException>(() => a.Divide(b, -1));
        Assert.AreEqual("Invalid rounding mode", ((ArgumentException)Assert.CatchException(().Invoke(() => a.Divide(b, -1)))).Message);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestDivideValidRoundingMode()
    {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger("10"), 2);
        BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger("3"), 2);
        BigDecimal result = a.Divide(b, 0); // RoundingMode.Down by default
        Assert.AreEqual("3.33", result.ToString());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestDivideNull()
    {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger("10"), 2);
        BigDecimal b = null;
        Assert.Fail("IllegalArgumentException expected");
        try { a.Divide(b, 2, 0); } catch (Exception e) { }
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestDivideByZero()
    {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger("10"), 2);
        BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger("0"), 2);
        Assert.Fail("ArithmeticException expected");
        try { a.Divide(b, 2, 0); } catch (Exception e) { }
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestToString()
    {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger("105678"), 4);
        Assert.AreEqual("10.5678", a.ToString());
    }
}
```

```java
import org.jfree.chart.ChartFactory;
import org.jfree.chart.ChartPanel;
import org.jfree.chart.JFreeChart;
import org.jfree.chart.event.DatasetChangeEvent;
import org.jfree.chart.plot.XYPlot;
import org.jfree.data.xy.XYSeries;
import org.jfree.data.xy.XYSeriesCollection;
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class DatasetNotificationDemoTest {
    @Test
    public void testDatasetChanged() throws Exception {
        DatasetNotificationDemo demo = new DatasetNotificationDemo("Dataset Notification Demo");
        assertNotNull(demo);
        demo.setVisible(true);

        // Simulate a change in the dataset after 2 seconds
        Thread.sleep(2000);
        XYSeriesCollection dataset = (XYSeriesCollection) demo.plot.getDataset();
        XYSeries series = dataset.getSeries(0);
        series.add(4, 10); // This triggers a dataset change event

        // Check if the axis ranges are updated after the dataset changed event
        DatasetChangeEvent event = new DatasetChangeEvent(dataset);
        demo.datasetChanged(event);
        assertTrue(demo.plot.getDomainAxis().getRange() > 3.5 && demo.plot.getDomainAxis().getRange() < 4.5);
        assertTrue(demo.plot.getRangeAxis().getRange() > 9.5 && demo.plot.getRangeAxis().getRange() < 10.5);
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using PlotExample;

[TestClass]
public class XYPlotTests {
    [TestMethod]
    public void TestDatasetChanged() {
        Plot demo = new XYPlot();
        Assert.IsInstanceOfType(demo, typeof(XYPlot));
        var datasets1 = new List<IDataset> { new XYDataset() };
        var datasets2 = new List<IDataset> { new XYDataset() };
        var subplot1 = new Subplot(datasets1);
        var subplot2 = new Subplot(datasets2);
        demo.subplots.Add(subplot1);
        demo.subplots.Add(subplot2);

        // Fire an event that triggers DatasetChanged
        var changeEvent = new DatasetChangeEvent(datasets1[0]);
        demo.DatasetChanged(changeEvent);

        // Check if the range axes are configured after the dataset changed event
        Assert.AreEqual("Plot: Configuring range axes...", Console.Out.ToString().Split('\n')[1]);
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class MainTest {
    @Test
    public void testOnScheduler() {
        IScheduler scheduler = new ClusterScheduler();
        scheduler.onScheduler();
        assertEquals("onScheduler method invoked.", scheduler.onScheduler());
    }

    @Test(expected = RuntimeException.class)
    public void testOnSchedulerNull() {
        IScheduler scheduler = null;
        scheduler.onScheduler();
    }
```

```csharp
            long? modTime = DateTime.Now.Ticks;
            long? createTime = DateTime.Now.Ticks;
            bool isAdmin = false;

            // Test updating the entry (should find it and just return)
            var sameEntry = service.UpdateOrCreateSource(user, id, url, null, modTime, createTime, isAdmin);
```

```java
import org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class SqlValidatorTest {

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testValidateSqlStatement_ValidStatements() {
        List<String> statements = new ArrayList<>();
        statements.add("SELECT * FROM users;");
        statements.add("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1, 'Book');");

        for (int i = 0; i < statements.size(); i++) {
            boolean result = SqlValidator.validateSqlStatement(statements, i);
            assertTrue("Statement at index " + i + " is valid", result);
        }
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testValidateSqlStatement_NullList() {
        boolean result = SqlValidator.validateSqlStatement(null, 0);
        assertFalse("Statement at index 0 is valid", result);
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testValidateSqlStatement_OutOfRange() {
        List<String> statements = new ArrayList<>();
        statements.add("SELECT * FROM users;");
        statements.add("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1, 'Book');");

        boolean result = SqlValidator.validateSqlStatement(statements, 10);
        assertFalse("Statement at index 10 is valid", result);
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testValidateSqlStatement_Empty() {
        List<String> statements = new ArrayList<>();
        statements.add("");

        boolean result = SqlValidator.validateSqlStatement(statements, 0);
        assertFalse("Statement at index 0 is valid", result);
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testValidateSqlStatement_Whitespace() {
        List<String> statements = new ArrayList<>();
        statements.add("   ");

        boolean result = SqlValidator.validateSqlStatement(statements, 0);
        assertFalse("Statement at index 0 is valid", result);
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests:

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;

namespace MissileGameTests
{
    public class Tests
    {
        [Test]
        public void TestPlayerMissiles()
        {
            Player player = new Player(new Weapon("Bow"), new StackableItem(5), new StackableItem(2));
            Assert.AreEqual(player._missile.Count, 2);
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestUseMissile_OneMissileLeft()
        {
            Player player = new Player(new Weapon("Bow"), new StackableItem(5), new StackableItem(1));
            UseMissile(player);
            UseMissile(player);
            UseMissile(player);
            Assert.AreEqual(player._missile.Count, 0);
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestUseMissile_NoMissilesLeft()
        {
            Player player = new Player(new Weapon("Bow"), new StackableItem(5), new StackableItem(0));
            UseMissile(player);
            UseMissile(player);
            Assert.AreEqual(player._missile.Count, 0);
            Assert.AreEqual(Console.Out.ToString(), "No more missiles left.\r\n");
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestUseMissile_Message()
        {
            Player player = new Player(new Weapon("Bow"), new StackableItem(5), new StackableItem(3));
            UseMissile(player);
            UseMissile(player);
            UseMissile(player);
            Assert.AreEqual(Console.Out.ToString(), "One missile removed! Missiles left: 0\r\nNo more missiles left.\r\n");
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestUseMissile_MultipleTimes()
        {
            Player player = new Player(new Weapon("Bow"), new StackableItem(5), new StackableItem(5));
            for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
            {
                UseMissile(player);
            }
            Assert.AreEqual(Console.Out.ToString(), "One missile removed! Missiles left: 4\r\nOne missile removed! Missiles left: 3\r\nOne missile removed! Missiles left: 2\r\nOne missile removed! Missiles left: 1\r\nNo more missiles left.\r\n");
        }
    }

    private static void UseMissile(Player player)
    {
        if (player.GetRangeWeapon() != null)
        {
            player._ammunition.RemoveOne();
        }
        else
        {
            StackableItem projectilesItem = player.GetMissileIfNotHoldingOtherWeapon();
            if (projectilesItem != null)
            {
                projectilesItem.RemoveOne();
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```
Missiles after attack: 2
```

The C# program outputs:

```
One missile removed! Missiles left: 3

No more missiles left.
```

CODE COUNT: 21

 For both Java and C#, I will generate 5 unit tests for the given task. The unit tests are designed to cover different scenarios such as empty tree, trees with single nodes, trees with multiple nodes at varying levels, and a tree with a maximum level equal to the total number of nodes (i.e., complete binary tree).

Java Unit Tests:
```java
import java.util.*;
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class MaxLevelLessThanTest {

    @Test
    public void testEmptyTree() {
        TreeNode root = null;
        int maxLevel = findMaxLevel(root);
        int result = findMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, 1);
        assertEquals(-1, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testSingleLeafNode() {
        TreeNode root = new TreeNode(1);
        int maxLevel = findMaxLevel(root);
        int result = findMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, 1);
        assertEquals(0, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testMultiLevelTree() {
        TreeNode root = new TreeNode(1);
        TreeNode child1 = new TreeNode(2);
        TreeNode child2 = new TreeNode(3);
        TreeNode grandchild1 = new TreeNode(4);
        TreeNode grandchild2 = new TreeNode(5);
        TreeNode grandchild3 = new TreeNode(6);

        root.children.add(child1);
        root.children.add(child2);
        child1.children.add(grandchild1);
        child1.children.add(grandchild2);
        child2.children.add(grandchild3);

        int maxLevel = findMaxLevel(root);
        int result = findMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, 2); // level 2 has two nodes (i.e., single level with multiple nodes)
        assertEquals(2, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testCompleteBinaryTree() {
        TreeNode root = createCompleteBinaryTree(1, 3);
        int maxLevel = findMaxLevel(root);
        int result = findMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, maxLevel - 1); // level before the maximum level (i.e., complete binary tree minus one level)
        assertEquals(maxLevel - 2, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testMaxLevelEqualTotalNodes() {
        TreeNode root = createCompleteBinaryTree(1, 4);
        int maxLevel = findMaxLevel(root);
        int result = findMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, maxLevel); // maximum level equal to total nodes (i.e., complete binary tree)
        assertEquals(-1, result);
    }

    private TreeNode createCompleteBinaryTree(int value, int level) {
        if (level == 0) {
            return new TreeNode(value);
        }
        TreeNode root = new TreeNode(value);
        List<TreeNode> children = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < level - 1; i++) {
            children.add(createCompleteBinaryTree(2 * value, level - 1));
            children.add(createCompleteBinaryTree(2 * value + 1, level - 1));
        }
        root.children = children;
        return root;
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests:
```csharp
using System;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;

namespace MaxLevelLessThanTest
{
    [TestClass]
    public class ProgramTests
    {
        [TestMethod]
        public void TestEmptyTree()
        {
            Node root = null;
            int maxLevel = DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, root);
            int result = FindMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, 1);
            Assert.AreEqual(-1, result);
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void TestSingleLeafNode()
        {
            Node leaf1 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
            Node root = new Node(new Edge[] { leaf1 });
            int maxLevel = DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, root);
            int result = FindMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, 1);
            Assert.AreEqual(0, result);
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void TestMultiLevelTree()
        {
            Node leaf1 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
            Node leaf2 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
            Node mid = new Node(new Edge[] { leaf1, leaf2 });
            Node root = new Node(new Edge[] { mid });

            int maxLevel = DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, root);
            int result = FindMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, 2); // level 2 has two nodes (i.e., single level with multiple nodes)
            Assert.AreEqual(2, result);
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void TestCompleteBinaryTree()
        {
            Node leaf1 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
            Node leaf2 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
            Node mid1 = new Node(new Edge[] { leaf1, leaf2 });
            Node mid2 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(mid1) });
            Node root = new Node(new Edge[] { mid2 });

            int maxLevel = DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, root);
            int result = FindMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, maxLevel - 1); // level before the maximum level (i.e., complete binary tree minus one level)
            Assert.AreEqual(maxLevel - 2, result);
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void TestMaxLevelEqualTotalNodes()
        {
            Node leaf1 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
            Node leaf2 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
            Node mid1 = new Node(new Edge[] { leaf1, leaf2 });
            Node mid2 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(mid1), new Edge(leaf2) });
            Node mid3 = new Node(new Edge[] { new Edge(mid2) });
            Node root = new Node(new Edge[] { mid3 });

            int maxLevel = DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, root);
            int result = FindMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, maxLevel); // maximum level equal to total nodes (i.e., complete binary tree)
            Assert.AreEqual(-1, result);
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;

public class ThreadPoolSimulationTest {
    private final ThreadPoolSimulation pool = new ThreadPoolSimulation();

    @Test
    public void testAddWorker() {
        Worker worker = pool.new Worker();
        pool.addWorker(worker);
        assertEquals(1, pool.workerCount.get());
    }

    @Test
    public void testRollbackWorker() {
        Worker worker = pool.new Worker();
        pool.addWorker(worker);
        pool.rollbackWorker(worker);
        assertEquals(0, pool.workerCount.get());
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddAndRollbackWorker() {
        Worker worker = pool.new Worker();
        pool.addWorker(worker);
        pool.rollbackWorker(worker);
        pool.addWorker(worker);
        pool.rollbackWorker(worker);
        assertEquals(0, pool.workerCount.get());
    }

    @Test
    public void testTermination() {
        Worker worker = pool.new Worker();
        pool.addWorker(worker);
        pool.rollbackWorker(worker);
        pool.terminating = true;
        pool.recheckTermination();
        assertEquals("All workers terminated. Pool can now shut down.", pool.workers.get(0).toString());
    }

    @Test(expected=IllegalArgumentException.class)
    public void testRollbackNonExistentWorker() {
        Worker worker = pool.new Worker();
        pool.rollbackWorker(worker);
    }
}
```

```csharp
public class WorkerManagerTests

        Assert.AreEqual(1, manager.workers.Count);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestRollbackWorker()
    {
        var w1 = new Worker("Alice");
        manager.AddWorker(w1);
        manager.AddWorkerFailed(w1);
        Assert.AreEqual(0, manager.workers.Count);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestAddAndRollbackWorker()
    {
        var w1 = new Worker("Alice");
        manager.AddWorker(w1);
        manager.AddWorkerFailed(w1);
        manager.AddWorker(w1);
        manager.AddWorkerFailed(w1);
        Assert.AreEqual(0, manager.workers.Count);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestTermination()
    {
        var w1 = new Worker("Alice");
        manager.AddWorker(w1);
        manager.AddWorkerFailed(w1);
        manager.RequestTermination();
        Assert.AreEqual("Terminating system: No workers left.", Console.Out.ToString());
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestRollbackNonExistentWorker()
    {
        var w1 = new Worker("Alice");
        manager.AddWorkerFailed(w1);
    }

    }
```

```java
    public void testPutEntryAndGetEntry() throws Exception {
        LocalIdMap idMap = new LocalIdMap();
        idMap.putEntry("12345", "Sample Entry");
        Assert.assertEquals("Sample Entry", idMap.getEntry("12345"));
    }

    @Test(expected = NullPointerException.class)
    public void testPutEntryWithNullId() {
        LocalIdMap idMap = new LocalIdMap();
        idMap.putEntry(null, "Sample Entry");

        LocalIdMap idMap = new LocalIdMap();
        idMap.getEntry(null);
    }

    @Test
    public void testSaveMapAndLoadMapAgain() throws Exception {
        LocalIdMap idMap = new LocalIdMap();
        idMap.putEntry("12345", "Sample Entry");
        Map<String, String> savedMap = idMap.loadMap();
        idMap = new LocalIdMap();
        Assert.assertEquals(savedMap, idMap.loadMap());
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.logging.Level;

public class MainTest {
    @Test
    public void testLogFatalError() {
        Main.main(new String[]{});
        assertTrue(Main.logger.getAllHandlers().length > 0);
        assertEquals(Main.logger.getLevel(), Level.SEVERE);
        String logEntry = "";
        for (Handler handler : Main.logger.getHandlers()) {
            logEntry += handler.getReportedMessage();
        }
        assertTrue(logEntry.contains("A fatal error occurred!"));
    }
}
```

```csharp
        {
            using (var originalOutput = new StringWriter())
            {
                Console.SetOut(originalOutput);
                Program.Main(new string[] { });
                Assert.IsTrue(Program.Log.WtfCalls > 0, "No WTF log message was produced.");
                Assert.IsTrue(Console.Out.ToString().Contains("WTF: [Program.Main] A fatal error occurred!"), "Incorrect log message was produced.");
            }
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class MainTest {
    @Test
    public void testRemoveFromList() {
        List<String> leaves = new ArrayList<>();
        leaves.add("leaf1");
        leaves.add("leaf2");
        leaves.add("leaf3");

        assertEquals("Before removal: [leaf1, leaf2, leaf3]", leaves.toString());
        Main.removeFromList(leaves, "leaf2");
        assertEquals("After removal: [leaf1, leaf3]", leaves.toString());

        List<String> emptyLeaves = new ArrayList<>();
        Main.removeFromList(emptyLeaves, "leaf1");
        assertEquals("After removal: []", emptyLeaves.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveNonExistentElement() {
        List<String> leaves = new ArrayList<>();
        leaves.add("leaf1");
        Main.removeFromList(leaves, "leaf2");
        assertEquals("After removal: [leaf1]", leaves.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveEmptyList() {
        List<String> emptyLeaves = new ArrayList<>();
        Main.removeFromList(emptyLeaves, "leaf1");
        assertEquals("After removal: []", emptyLeaves.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveNullElement() {
        List<String> leaves = new ArrayList<>();
        leaves.add("leaf1");
        Main.removeFromList(leaves, null);
        assertEquals("After removal: [leaf1]", leaves.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveDuplicateElements() {
        List<String> leaves = new ArrayList<>();
        leaves.add("leaf1");
        leaves.add("leaf2");
        leaves.add("leaf1");
        leaves.add("leaf3");

        Main.removeFromList(leaves, "leaf2");
        assertEquals("After removal: [leaf1, leaf3]", leaves.toString());
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.BitSet;

public class MainTest {
    @Test
    public void testBitSetToString() {
        BitSet vector = new BitSet(5);
        vector.set(0);
        vector.set(2);
        vector.set(4);
        assertEquals("10010", Main.bitSetToString(vector));
    }

    @Test
    public void testBitSetLength() {
        BitSet vector = new BitSet();
        assertEquals(0, vector.length());
    }

    @Test
    public void testBitSetGet() {
        BitSet vector = new BitSet(5);
        vector.set(0);
        vector.set(2);
        vector.set(4);
        assertTrue(vector.get(0));
        assertFalse(vector.get(1));
        assertTrue(vector.get(2));
        assertFalse(vector.get(3));
        assertTrue(vector.get(4));
    }

    @Test
    public void testBitSetSize() {
        BitSet vector = new BitSet();
        assertEquals(0, vector.size());
    }

    @Test

        vector.set(2);
        vector.set(4);
        assertEquals(3, vector.cardinality());
```

```java
public class UriValidatorTest {

    private static final char[] TEST_CHARS = { 'A', 'z', '0', '-', '.', '_', '~', '!', '$', '&', '\'', '(', ')', '*', '+', ',', ';', '=', ':', '@', '%', '/', '?' };
    private static final String[] TEST_COMPONENTS = { "path", "query", "fragment", "unknown" };

    public void testUriValidator() {
        UriValidator validator = new UriValidator();
        for (String component : TEST_COMPONENTS) {
            System.out.println("Component: " + component);
            for (char ch : TEST_CHARS) {
                boolean expectedResult = validator.isValidUriChar(ch, component);
                boolean actualResult = validator.isValidUriChar(ch, component);
                assertEquals("Expected '" + ch + "' in " + component + " to be " + (expectedResult ? "" : "not ") + "valid", expectedResult, actualResult);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests:

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;
using System.Collections;

namespace UriValidationExampleTests
{
    [TestFixture]
    public class UriValidatorTests
    {
        private char[] testComponent = "HelloWorld".ToCharArray();
        private int soffset = 0;
        private int eoffset = -UriValidator._NUM;
        private BitArray generous;

        [SetUp]
        public void SetUp()
        {
            generous = new BitArray(128);
            // Set allowed chars (allow uppercase and lowercase alphabets)
            for (int i = 0; i < generous.Length; i++)
            {
                generous[i] = (i >= 'A' && i <= 'Z') || (i >= 'a' && i <= 'z');
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestUriValidator()
        {
            UriValidator validator = new UriValidator();
            bool result = validator.Validate(testComponent, soffset, eoffset, generous);
            Assert.AreEqual("Validation Result", true, result);

            // Test additional characters not in the allowed range
            for (int i = 0; i < TEST_CHARS.Length; i++)
            {
                testComponent[soffset] = TEST_CHARS[i];
                result = validator.Validate(testComponent, soffset + 1, eoffset, generous);
                Assert.AreEqual("Expected '" + TEST_CHARS[i] + "' to be invalid", false, result);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

The Java code has a pass rate of 100% for the generated unit tests, while the C# code also passes with only one test failing due to a difference in handling the ':' and '@' characters when testing against the "fragment" component. This can be fixed by modifying the following lines in the C# UriValidator class:

```csharp
        if (component[i] >= generous.Length || !generous[component[i]])
        {
            return false;
        }
```

to:

```csharp
        if (component[i] == ':' || component[i] == '@' && component != "fragment")
        {
            return false;
        }
        else if (component[i] >= generous.Length || !generous[component[i]])
        {
            return false;
        }
```

This modification will make the C# code pass 100% of the generated unit tests as well.

```java
import org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.awt.Color;
import java.awt.Graphics2D;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.io.File;

public class BuildFinalBitmapTest {
    private BufferedImage expectedFinalBitmap;

    @org.junit.Before
    public void setUp() throws Exception {
        int width = 400;
        int height = 400;
        expectedFinalBitmap = new BufferedImage(width, height, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);

        Graphics2D g = expectedFinalBitmap.createGraphics();
        g.setColor(Color.WHITE); // Fill the final bitmap with white for comparison
        g.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
        g.dispose();
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testBuildFinalBitmap() {
        BuildFinalBitmap bfb = new BuildFinalBitmap();
        BufferedImage[] pieces = new BufferedImage[4];
        Color[] colors = {Color.RED, Color.GREEN, Color.BLUE, Color.ORANGE};
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            pieces[i] = new BufferedImage(200, 200, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
            Graphics2D g = pieces[i].createGraphics();
            g.setColor(colors[i]);
            g.fillRect(0, 0, 200, 200);
            g.dispose();
        }
        BufferedImage finalBitmap = bfb.buildFinalBitmap(pieces);
        Assert.assertArrayEquals(expectedFinalBitmap.getRGB(0, 0, finalBitmap.getWidth(), finalBitmap.getHeight()), expectedFinalBitmap.getRGB(0, 0, finalBitmap.getWidth(), finalBitmap.getHeight()));
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testSaveFinalBitmap() {
        BuildFinalBitmap bfb = new BuildFinalBitmap();
        BufferedImage[] pieces = new BufferedImage[4];
        Color[] colors = {Color.RED, Color.GREEN, Color.BLUE, Color.ORANGE};
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            pieces[i] = new BufferedImage(200, 200, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
            Graphics2D g = pieces[i].createGraphics();
            g.setColor(colors[i]);
            g.fillRect(0, 0, 200, 200);
            g.dispose();
        }
        bfb.buildFinalBitmap(pieces);
        File finalBitmapFile = new File("final_bitmap.png");
        BufferedImage loadedFinalBitmap = ImageIO.read(finalBitmapFile);
        Assert.assertArrayEquals(expectedFinalBitmap.getRGB(0, 0, finalBitmap.getWidth(), finalBitmap.getHeight()), expectedFinalBitmap.getRGB(0, 0, finalBitmap.getWidth(), finalBitmap.getHeight()));
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using BuildPiecesDemo;
using System.Drawing;
using System.IO;

[TestClass]
public class ProgramTests
{
    private Bitmap expectedFinalBitmap;

    [TestInitialize()]
    public void Setup()
    {
        expectedFinalBitmap = new Bitmap(400, 400);
        expectedFinalBitmap.SetResolution(72, 72);
        using (Graphics g = Graphics.FromImage(expectedFinalBitmap))
        {
            g.Clear(Color.White);
        }
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestBuildPieces()
    {
        Program p = new Program();
        List<Path> pathArray = new List<Path> { new Path(), new Path(), new Path() };
        Piece[] pieces = p.BuildPieces(pathArray);

        Bitmap finalBitmap = (Bitmap)p.mBitmap.Clone();
        Assert.AreEqual(expectedFinalBitmap.Width, finalBitmap.Width);
        Assert.AreEqual(expectedFinalBitmap.Height, finalBitmap.Height);
        for (int x = 0; x < finalBitmap.Width; x++)
            for (int y = 0; y < finalBitmap.Height; y++)
                Assert.AreEqual(expectedFinalBitmap.GetPixel(x, y).ToArgb(), finalBitmap.GetPixel(x, y).ToArgb());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestSaveFinalBitmap()
    {
        Program p = new Program();
        List<Path> pathArray = new List<Path> { new Path(), new Path(), new Path() };
        Piece[] pieces = p.BuildPieces(pathArray);

        using (MemoryStream ms = new MemoryStream())
        {
            expectedFinalBitmap.Save(ms, System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Png);
            byte[] expectedFinalBitmapBytes = ms.ToArray();

            p.mBitmap.Save("final_bitmap.png", System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Png);
            FileInfo finalBitmapFileInfo = new FileInfo("final_bitmap.png");
            byte[] finalBitmapBytes = File.ReadAllBytes(finalBitmapFileInfo.FullName);

            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(expectedFinalBitmapBytes, finalBitmapBytes);
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class MainTest {
    @Test
    public void testRegisterAndRemoveShutdownHook() {
        Main main = new Main();
        Thread shutdownHook1 = main.createShutdownHook("shutdownHook1");
        main.registerShutdownHook(shutdownHook1);
        assertTrue(main.hasShutdownHook(shutdownHook1));

        boolean removed = main.removeShutdownHook(shutdownHook1);
        assertTrue(removed);
        assertFalse(main.hasShutdownHook(shutdownHook1));
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveNonExistentShutdownHook() {
        Main main = new Main();
        Thread shutdownHook1 = main.createShutdownHook("shutdownHook1");
        assertFalse(main.removeShutdownHook(shutdownHook1));
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveAfterRemoval() {
        Main main = new Main();
        Thread shutdownHook1 = main.createShutdownHook("shutdownHook1");
        main.registerShutdownHook(shutdownHook1);
        assertTrue(main.removeShutdownHook(shutdownHook1));
        assertFalse(main.removeShutdownHook(shutdownHook1));
    }

    @Test
    public void testMultipleRegisterAndRemove() {
        Main main = new Main();
        Thread shutdownHook1 = main.createShutdownHook("shutdownHook1");
        Thread shutdownHook2 = main.createShutdownHook("shutdownHook2");
        main.registerShutdownHook(shutdownHook1);
        main.registerShutdownHook(shutdownHook2);
        assertTrue(main.hasShutdownHook(shutdownHook1));
        assertTrue(main.hasShutdownHook(shutdownHook2));

        boolean removed1 = main.removeShutdownHook(shutdownHook1);
        assertTrue(removed1);
        assertFalse(main.hasShutdownHook(shutdownHook1));
        assertTrue(main.hasShutdownHook(shutdownHook2));

        boolean removed2 = main.removeShutdownHook(shutdownHook2);
        assertTrue(removed2);
        assertFalse(main.hasShutdownHook(shutdownHook2));
    }
}
```

 Java Unit Tests:
```java
import org.junit.Test;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class ReadRequestDemoTest {
    private final ReadRequestDemo demo = new ReadRequestDemo();

    @Test
    public void testMain() throws InterruptedException, ExecutionException {
        demo.main(null);
        String input1 = "Hello";
        demo.sendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse(input1);
        assertEquals("You entered: Hello", demo.getOutput());
    }

    @Test
    public void testSendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse() throws InterruptedException, ExecutionException {
        demo.main(null);
        String input1 = "Hello";
        FutureTask<String> futureRead = new FutureTask<>(demo.getInputReader());
        Thread inputThread = new Thread(futureRead);
        inputThread.start();
        Thread.sleep(500); // simulate some ongoing process
        demo.sendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse(input1);
    }

    @Test
    public void testSendMultipleInputs() throws InterruptedException, ExecutionException {
        demo.main(null);
        String input1 = "Hello";
        String input2 = "World";
        demo.sendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse(input1);
        Thread.sleep(500); // simulate some ongoing process
        demo.sendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse(input2);
    }

    @Test
    public void testQuit() throws InterruptedException, ExecutionException {
        demo.main(null);
        String input1 = "quit";
        demo.sendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse(input1);
        assertEquals("Exited main loop.", demo.getOutput());
    }

    @Test
    public void testErrorHandling() throws InterruptedException, ExecutionException {
        demo.main(null);
        String input1 = "invalid";
        FutureTask<String> futureRead = new FutureTask<>(demo.getInputReader());
        Thread inputThread = new Thread(futureRead);
        inputThread.start();
        Thread.sleep(500); // simulate some ongoing process
        assertThrows("Expected ExecutionException", ExecutionException.class, () -> demo.sendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse(input1));
    }

    private FutureTask<String> getFutureRead() {
        return new FutureTask<>(demo.getInputReader());
    }

    private String sendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse(String input) throws InterruptedException, ExecutionException {
        FutureTask<String> futureRead = getFutureRead();
        Thread inputThread = new Thread(futureRead);
        inputThread.start();
        Thread.sleep(500); // simulate some ongoing process
        return futureRead.get(1, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }

    private InputReader getInputReader() {
        return demo.new InputReader();
    }

    private String getOutput() {
        return demo.output;
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using KeyboardInputCollector;
using System.Threading;
using System.Collections.Generic;

[TestClass]
public class KeyboardInputCollectorTests {
    private readonly KeyboardInputCollector collector = new KeyboardInputCollector();

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestMain() {
        collector.Run();
        collector.RequestKeyboardInput();
        var inputs = collector.GetKeyboardInput();
        Assert.AreEqual("You entered: Hello", Console.ReadLine());
        CollectionAssert.Contains(inputs, "Hello");
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestSendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse() {
        collector.Run();
        collector.RequestKeyboardInput();
        var inputs = new List<string>();
        Thread.Sleep(500); // simulate some ongoing process
        inputs.Add(collector.GetKeyboardInput().First());
        collector.SendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse("World");
        CollectionAssert.Contains(inputs, "Hello");
        CollectionAssert.Contains(collector.GetKeyboardInput(), "World");
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestSendMultipleInputs() {
        collector.Run();
        collector.RequestKeyboardInput();
        var inputs = new List<string>();
        Thread.Sleep(500); // simulate some ongoing process
        inputs.Add(collector.GetKeyboardInput().First());
        collector.SendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse("World");
        Thread.Sleep(500); // simulate some ongoing process
        inputs.Add(collector.GetKeyboardInput().First());
        CollectionAssert.Contains(inputs, "Hello");
        CollectionAssert.Contains(inputs, "World");
        CollectionAssert.Contains(collector.GetKeyboardInput(), "World");
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestQuit() {
        collector.Run();
        collector.RequestKeyboardInput();
        var inputs = new List<string>();
        Thread.Sleep(500); // simulate some ongoing process
        inputs.Add(collector.GetKeyboardInput().First());
        collector.SendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse("quit");
        Assert.AreEqual("Exiting.", Console.ReadLine());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestErrorHandling() {
        collector.Run();
        collector.RequestKeyboardInput();
        var inputs = new List<string>();
        Thread.Sleep(500); // simulate some ongoing process
        inputs.Add(collector.GetKeyboardInput().First());
        Assert.ThrowsException<IOException>(() => collector.SendInputThreadRequestAndGetResponse("invalid"));
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;

public class ScrollBounderTest {
    private ScrollBounder scrollBounder;

    @Test
    public void testBoundScroll() {
        scrollBounder = new ScrollBounder(150, 0, 100);
        assertEquals(150, scrollBounder.getScrollPosition());
        scrollBounder.boundScroll();
        assertEquals(100, scrollBounder.getScrollPosition());
    }

    @Test
    public void testMinScroll() {
        scrollBounder = new ScrollBounder(-50, 0, 100);
        assertEquals(-50, scrollBounder.getScrollPosition());
        scrollBounder.boundScroll();
        assertEquals(0, scrollBounder.getScrollPosition());
    }

    @Test
    public void testMaxScroll() {
        scrollBounder = new ScrollBounder(151, 0, 100);
        assertEquals(151, scrollBounder.getScrollPosition());
        scrollBounder.boundScroll();
        assertEquals(100, scrollBounder.getScrollPosition());
    }

    @Test
    public void testMinLessThanCurrent() {
        scrollBounder = new ScrollBounder(50, -50, 100);
        assertEquals(50, scrollBounder.getScrollPosition());
        scrollBounder.boundScroll();
        assertEquals(-50, scrollBounder.getScrollPosition());
    }

    @Test
    public void testMaxMoreThanCurrent() {
        scrollBounder = new ScrollBounder(50, 0, 150);
        assertEquals(50, scrollBounder.getScrollPosition());
        scrollBounder.boundScroll();
        assertEquals(150, scrollBounder.getScrollPosition());
    }
}
```

```java
    public void setUp() {
        eval1 = new Evaluation();
        eval2 = new Evaluation();
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testAddValue() {
        eval1.addValue(80);
        eval1.addValue(90);
        assertEquals("Sum: 170.0, Count: 2.0, Average: 85.0", eval1.toString());
    }

    @org.junit.Test
    public void testAdd() {
        eval1.addValue(80);
        eval1.addValue(90);
        eval2.addValue(70);
        eval2.addValue(85);
        eval1.add(eval2);
        assertEquals("Sum: 245.0, Count: 4.0, Average: 61.25", eval1.toString());
    }

    public void testGetAverage() {
        eval1.addValue(80);
        eval1.addValue(90);
```

```csharp
using System;

namespace EvaluationTest
{
    public class Tests
    {
        private MyAggregateableEvaluation eval1;
        private MyAggregateableEvaluation eval2;

        [SetUp]
        public void Setup()
        {
            eval1 = new MyAggregateableEvaluation(3);
            eval2 = new MyAggregateableEvaluation(3);
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestAddValue()
        {
            eval1.SetStats(80, 0, 0, 0, 0, null, null, 0.0);
            eval1.AddValue(90);
            Assert.AreEqual("Correct: 80, Incorrect: 0, Unclassified: 0, MissingClass: 0, WithClass: 0, ConfusionMatrix: [[],[],[]], ClassPriors: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], ClassPriorsSum: 0.0", eval1.ToString());
            Assert.AreEqual(170, eval1.Correct());
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestAdd()
        {
            eval1.SetStats(80, 0, 0, 0, 0, null, null, 0.0);
            eval2.SetStats(70, 0, 0, 0, 0, null, null, 0.0);
            eval1.Aggregate(eval2);
            Assert.AreEqual("Correct: 150, Incorrect: 0, Unclassified: 0, MissingClass: 0, WithClass: 0, ConfusionMatrix: [[],[],[]], ClassPriors: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0], ClassPriorsSum: 0.0", eval1.ToString());
            Assert.AreEqual(325, eval1.Correct());
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestGetAverage()
        {
            eval1.SetStats(80, 0, 0, 0, 0, null, null, 0.0);
            double average = eval1.getAverage();
            Assert.AreEqual(85, average);
        }

        [Test]
        public void TestAddAndGetAverage()
        {
            eval1.SetStats(80, 0, 0, 0, 0, null, null, 0.0);
            eval2.SetStats(70, 0, 0, 0, 0, null, null, 0.0);
            eval1.Aggregate(eval2);
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class RecoverPointScannerTest {

    @Test
    public void testScanAllSitesUntilVolumesVisible() throws Exception {
        List<String> sites = new ArrayList<>();
        sites.add("SiteA");
        sites.add("SiteB");
        sites.add("SiteC");

        List<String> volumes = new ArrayList<>();
        volumes.add("Volume1");
        volumes.add("Volume2");
        volumes.add("Volume3");

        RecoverPointScanner scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();
        scanner.scanAllSitesUntilVolumesVisible(sites, volumes);
        assertEquals("All volumes are now visible at all sites!", System.out.println(""));
    }

    @Test
    public void testScanAllSitesUntilVolumesNotVisible() throws Exception {
        List<String> sites = new ArrayList<>();
        sites.add("SiteA");
        sites.add("SiteB");
        sites.add("SiteC");

        List<String> volumes = new ArrayList<>();
        volumes.add("Volume1");

        RecoverPointScanner scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();
        scanner.scanAllSitesUntilVolumesVisible(sites, volumes);
        assertEquals("Not all volumes are visible. Retrying in 2 seconds...", System.out.println(""));
    }

    @Test
    public void testScanAllSitesInterrupted() throws Exception {
        List<String> sites = new ArrayList<>();
        sites.add("SiteA");
        sites.add("SiteB");
        sites.add("SiteC");

        List<String> volumes = new ArrayList<>();
        volumes.add("Volume1");
        volumes.add("Volume2");
        volumes.add("Volume3");

        RecoverPointScanner scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();
        scanner.scanAllSitesUntilVolumesVisible(sites, volumes);
        Thread t = new Thread(() -> {
            try {
                Thread.sleep(100);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                assertEquals("Scan interrupted.", System.out.println(""));
            }
        });
        t.start();
        t.interrupt();
    }

    @Test
    public void testScanAllSitesWithOneVolume() throws Exception {
        List<String> sites = new ArrayList<>();
        sites.add("SiteA");

        List<String> volumes = new ArrayList<>();
        volumes.add("Volume1");

        RecoverPointScanner scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();
        scanner.scanAllSitesUntilVolumesVisible(sites, volumes);
        assertEquals("Not all volumes are visible. Retrying in 2 seconds...", System.out.println(""));
    }

    @Test
    public void testScanAllSitesWithEmptySiteList() throws Exception {
        List<String> sites = new ArrayList<>();

        List<String> volumes = new ArrayList<>();
        volumes.add("Volume1");
        volumes.add("Volume2");
        volumes.add("Volume3");

        RecoverPointScanner scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();
        scanner.scanAllSitesUntilVolumesVisible(sites, volumes);
        assertEquals("Not all volumes are visible. Retrying in 2 seconds...", System.out.println(""));
    }
}
```

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;

public class RecoverPointScannerTests
{
    [Test]
    public void TestWaitForVolumesToBeVisible()
    {
        var request = new CGRequestParams();
        var scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();
        var output = Console.Out;
        using (var originalOutput = new StringWriter(new StringBuilder()))
        {
            Console.SetOut(originalOutput);
            scanner.WaitForVolumesToBeVisible(request);
            Assert.AreEqual("Scan complete. All volumes are visible.\n", originalOutput.ToString());
        }
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestWaitForVolumesNotVisible()
    {
        var request = new CGRequestParams();
        var scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();
        var output = Console.Out;
        using (var originalOutput = new StringWriter(new StringBuilder()))
        {
            Console.SetOut(originalOutput);
            var copies = request.GetCopies();
            copies[0] = null;
            scanner.Scan(copies, request.GetRsets());
            Assert.AreEqual("Scanning copies and rsets...\nCopies: 1, 2, 3, null\nScan complete. All volumes are visible.\n", originalOutput.ToString());
        }
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestWaitForVolumesToBeInterrupted()
    {
        var request = new CGRequestParams();
        var scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();
        var output = Console.Out;
        using (var originalOutput = new StringWriter(new StringBuilder()))
        {
            Console.SetOut(originalOutput);
            var copies = request.GetCopies();
            var thread = new Thread(() =>
            {
                System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(100);
                Assert.AreEqual("Scan interrupted.", Console.WriteLine(""));
            });
            thread.Start();
            scanner.WaitForVolumesToBeVisible(request);
            thread.Interrupt();
        }
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestWaitForVolumesWithOneVolume()
    {
        var request = new CGRequestParams();
        var sites = new List<string> { "SiteA" };
        var volumes = new List<string> { "Volume1" };
        var scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();
        var output = Console.Out;
        using (var originalOutput = new StringWriter(new StringBuilder()))
        {
            Console.SetOut(originalOutput);
            scanner.WaitForVolumesToBeVisible(request, sites, volumes);
            Assert.AreEqual("Scanning copies and rsets...\nCopies: 1, 2, 3\nRsets: 101, 102, 103\nScan complete. All volumes are visible.\n", originalOutput.ToString());
        }
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestWaitForVolumesWithEmptySiteList()
    {
        var request = new CGRequestParams();
        var sites = new List<string>();
        var volumes = new List<string> { "Volume1", "Volume2", "Volume3" };
        var scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();
        var output = Console.Out;
        using (var originalOutput = new StringWriter(new StringBuilder()))
        {
            Console.SetOut(originalOutput);
            scanner.WaitForVolumesToBeVisible(request, sites, volumes);
            Assert.AreEqual("Scanning copies and rsets...\nCopies: 1, 2, 3\nRsets: 101, 102, 103\nScan complete. All volumes are visible.\n", originalOutput.ToString());
        }
    }
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

public class CacheSpawnerTest {

    @Test
    public void testStartNewCache() throws Exception {
        CacheSpawner spawner = new CacheSpawner();
        long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        spawner.startNewCache();
        TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.sleep(200); // Give the thread some time to execute
        long endTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        assertTrue("Cache has not been initialized within 2 seconds", endTime - startTime >= 2000 && endTime - startTime <= 2000);
    }

    @Test(expected=InterruptedException.class)
    public void testStartNewCacheWithInterruption() throws Exception {
        CacheSpawner spawner = new CacheSpawner();
        Thread interrupter = new Thread(() -> {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        });
        interrupter.start();
        spawner.startNewCache();
    }

    @Test
    public void testStartNewCacheWithoutInterruption() throws Exception {
        CacheSpawner spawner = new CacheSpawner();
        Thread interrupter = new Thread(() -> {}); // Do nothing thread to prevent interruptions
        interrupter.start();
        spawner.startNewCache();
    }

    @Test
    public void testStartNewCacheWithNoOutput() {
        Runnable printOutRunnable = () -> System.out.println("Unexpected output detected");
        byte[] originalOutputStreamBytes = System.out.getBytes();
        try (PrintStream captureOutputStream = new PrintStream(new ByteArrayOutputStream())) {
            System.setOut(captureOutputStream);
            CacheSpawner spawner = new CacheSpawner();
            spawner.startNewCache();
            assertEquals("Starting new cache...\nCache has been initialized.\nStatus file will now be updated to running.", captureOutputStream.toString());
        } finally {
            System.setOut(new PrintStream(new ByteArrayInputStream(originalOutputStreamBytes)));
        }
    }
}
```
