```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using ExportMaskExample;

[TestClass]
public class ProgramTests
{
    [TestMethod]
    public void TestGetExportMasksForUris()
    {
        HashSet<Uri> uris = new HashSet<Uri> { new Uri("http://example.com/mask/1"), new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3"), new Uri("http://example.com/mask/nonexistent") };
        var expected = new Dictionary<Uri, ExportMask>
        {
            { new Uri("http://example.com/mask/1"), new ExportMask(new Uri("http://example.com/mask/1")) },
            { new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3"), new ExportMask(new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3")) }
        };
        var dbClient = new DbClient(expected);
        var program = new Program(dbClient);
        var result = program.CreateExportMaskMap(uris);
        CollectionAssert.AreEqual(expected, result);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestEmptyUriSet()
    {
        HashSet<Uri> uris = new HashSet<Uri>();
        var dbClient = new DbClient(new Dictionary<Uri, ExportMask>());
        var program = new Program(dbClient);
        var result = program.CreateExportMaskMap(uris);
        Assert.IsTrue(result.Count == 0);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestUriNotInDatabase()
    {
        HashSet<Uri> uris = new HashSet<Uri> { new Uri("http://example.com/mask/nonexistent") };
        var dbClient = new DbClient(new Dictionary<Uri, ExportMask>
        {
            { new Uri("http://example.com/mask/1"), new ExportMask(new Uri("http://example.com/mask/1")) },
            { new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3"), new ExportMask(new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3")) }
        });
        var program = new Program(dbClient);
        var result = program.CreateExportMaskMap(uris);
        Assert.IsTrue(result.Count == 0);
    }
}
```

 Java Unit Tests:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.Vector;

public class MainTest {
    @Test
    public void testInsertElementAt() {
        Vector<int[]> vectorOfArrays = new Vector<>();
        int[] arrayToInsert = {1, 2, 3, 4};
        int index = 0;

        vectorOfArrays.insertElementAt(arrayToInsert, index);

        int[] expectedArray = {arrayToInsert};
        assertEquals("Vector content", expectedArray, vectorOfArrays.toArray());
    }

    @Test
    public void testEmptyVector() {
        Vector<int[]> vectorOfArrays = new Vector<>();
        int index = 0;
        int[] arrayToInsert = {1, 2, 3, 4};

        vectorOfArrays.insertElementAt(arrayToInsert, index);

        int[] expectedArray = {arrayToInsert};
        assertEquals("Vector content", expectedArray, vectorOfArrays.toArray());
    }

    @Test
    public void testInsertAtEnd() {
        Vector<int[]> vectorOfArrays = new Vector<>();
        int[] arrayToInsert = {1, 2, 3, 4};
        int index = vectorOfArrays.size();

        vectorOfArrays.insertElementAt(arrayToInsert, index);

        int[] expectedArray = {vectorOfArrays.isEmpty() ? arrayToInsert : vectorOfArrays.get(0)};
        assertEquals("Vector content", expectedArray, vectorOfArrays.toArray());
    }

    @Test
    public void testOverwriteExistingIndex() {
        Vector<int[]> vectorOfArrays = new Vector<>();
        int[] arrayToInsert = {1, 2, 3, 4};
        int index = 0;

        vectorOfArrays.add(arrayToInsert);
        vectorOfArrays.set(index, new int[]{99});

        int[] expectedArray = new int[]{new int[]{99}};
        assertEquals("Vector content", expectedArray, vectorOfArrays.toArray());
    }

    @Test
    public void testInsertNegativeIndex() {
        Vector<int[]> vectorOfArrays = new Vector<>();
        int[] arrayToInsert = {1, 2, 3, 4};
        int index = -1;

        Assert.assertThrows("Expected ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException", ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException.class, () -> {
            vectorOfArrays.insertElementAt(arrayToInsert, index);
        });
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests:

```csharp
using System;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using static DataUtils;
using static ImmutableArray2<int>;

[TestClass]
public class ProgramTests
{
    [TestMethod]
    public void TestInsertElementAt()
    {
        int[] arr = new int[] { 1, 2, 3 };
        var immutable = new ImmutableArray2<int>((int[])arr.Clone(), arr.Length, true);
        var inserted = immutable.Insert(1, 99);
        var inserted2 = inserted.Insert(4, 88); // new entry at the end (simulate extended length)

        Assert.AreEqual("Original", "[1, 2, 3]", immutable.ToString());
        Assert.AreEqual("After Insert at index 1", "[1, 99, 2, 3]", inserted.ToString());
        Assert.AreEqual("After Insert at index 4", $"[1, 99, 2, 3, {88}, 4]", inserted2.ToString());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestEmptyArray()
    {
        var immutable = new ImmutableArray2<int>(new int[]{}, 0, true);
        var inserted = immutable.Insert(0, 99);

        Assert.AreEqual("Original", "[]", immutable.ToString());
        Assert.AreEqual("After Insert at index 0", "[99]", inserted.ToString());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestInsertAtEnd()
    {
        int[] arr = new int[] { 1, 2, 3 };
        var immutable = new ImmutableArray2<int>((int[])arr.Clone(), arr.Length, true);
        var inserted = immutable.Insert(arr.Length, 99); // insert at the end (simulate extended length)

        Assert.AreEqual("Original", "[1, 2, 3]", immutable.ToString());
        Assert.AreEqual("After Insert at index 4", $"[1, 2, 3, {arr.Length}, 99]", inserted.ToString());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestOverwriteExistingIndex()
    {
        int[] arr = new int[] { 1, 2, 3 };
        var immutable = new ImmutableArray2<int>((int[])arr.Clone(), arr.Length, true);
        var inserted = immutable.Insert(0, 99);
        inserted = inserted.Insert(1, 88); // overwrite existing index

        Assert.AreEqual("Original", "[1, 2, 3]", immutable.ToString());
        Assert.AreEqual("After Insert at index 0", "[99, 88, 2, 3]", inserted.ToString());
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestInsertNegativeIndex()
    {
        int[] arr = new int[] { 1, 2, 3 };
        var immutable = new ImmutableArray2<int>((int[])arr.Clone(), arr.Length, true);
        Assert.ThrowsException<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => {
            immutable.Insert(-1, 99);
        });
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.awt.Toolkit;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.io.File;

public class ImagePrintDemoTest {
    private final String TEST_IMAGE_PATH = "test_image.jpg";

    @Test
    public void testPrintImage() throws Exception {
        BufferedImage testImage = (BufferedImage) Toolkit.getDefaultToolkit().getImage(getClass().getResource(TEST_IMAGE_PATH));
        Image image = new ImageIcon(TEST_IMAGE_PATH).getImage(); // Replace with your image path
        PrinterJob job = PrinterJob.getPrinterJob();
        ImagePrintDemo printable = new ImagePrintDemo(image);
        job.setPrintable(printable, null);
        boolean doPrint = job.printDialog();
        if (doPrint) {
            try {
                File file = File.createTempFile("test", ".png");
                job.print(file.toURI().toURL(), null);
                BufferedImage outputImage = ImageIO.read(file);
                assertEquals(testImage.getWidth(), outputImage.getWidth());
                assertEquals(testImage.getHeight(), outputImage.getHeight());
            } catch (PrinterException | IOException e) {
                fail("Failed to print and save image: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        } else {
            fail("Print dialog was not shown");
        }
    }

    @Test
    public void testPrintDifferentImage() throws Exception {
        BufferedImage testImage1 = (BufferedImage) Toolkit.getDefaultToolkit().getImage(getClass().getResource(TEST_IMAGE_PATH));
        BufferedImage testImage2 = ImageIO.read(new File("different_test_image.jpg")); // Replace with your image path

        Image image1 = new ImageIcon(TEST_IMAGE_PATH).getImage();
        Image image2 = new ImageIcon("different_test_image.jpg").getImage();
        PrinterJob job = PrinterJob.getPrinterJob();
        ImagePrintDemo printable1 = new ImagePrintDemo(image1);
        ImagePrintDemo printable2 = new ImagePrintDemo(image2);
        job.setPrintable(printable1, null);
        boolean doPrint1 = job.printDialog();
        if (doPrint1) {
            try {
                File file1 = File.createTempFile("test", ".png");
                job.print(file1.toURI().toURL(), null);
                BufferedImage outputImage1 = ImageIO.read(file1);
                assertEquals(testImage1.getWidth(), outputImage1.getWidth());
                assertEquals(testImage1.getHeight(), outputImage1.getHeight());
            } catch (PrinterException | IOException e) {
                fail("Failed to print and save image: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        } else {
            fail("Print dialog was not shown");
        }

        job.setPrintable(printable2, null);
        boolean doPrint2 = job.printDialog();
        if (doPrint2) {
            try {
                File file2 = File.createTempFile("test", ".png");
                job.print(file2.toURI().toURL(), null);
                BufferedImage outputImage2 = ImageIO.read(file2);
                assertEquals(testImage2.getWidth(), outputImage2.getWidth());
                assertEquals(testImage2.getHeight(), outputImage2.getHeight());
            } catch (PrinterException | IOException e) {
                fail("Failed to print and save image: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        } else {
            fail("Print dialog was not shown");
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.IO;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Xunit;

[TestFixture]
public class PrintoutTests {
    private const string TEST_IMAGE_PATH = "test_image.jpg";

    [Test]
    public void TestPrintImage() {
        Bitmap testImage = new Bitmap(TEST_IMAGE_PATH);
        Bitmap img = new Bitmap(100, 50);
        using (Graphics g = Graphics.FromImage(img))
        {
            g.Clear(Color.Red);
            g.DrawRectangle(Pens.Black, 10, 10, 80, 30);
        }
        Printout printout = new Printout();
        Component comp = new Component();
        printout.WriteNoScale(img, comp);
        Bitmap outputImage = (Bitmap)printout.page;
        Assert.AreEqual(testImage.Width, outputImage.Width);
        Assert.AreEqual(testImage.Height, outputImage.Height);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestPrintDifferentImage() {
        Bitmap testImage1 = new Bitmap(TEST_IMAGE_PATH);
        Bitmap testImage2 = new Bitmap("different_test_image.jpg"); // Replace with your image path
        Bitmap img1 = new Bitmap(100, 50);
        Bitmap img2 = new Bitmap(200, 300);
        using (Graphics g = Graphics.FromImage(img1))
        {
            g.Clear(Color.Red);
            g.DrawRectangle(Pens.Black, 10, 10, 80, 30);
        }
        using (Graphics g = Graphics.FromImage(img2))
        {
            g.Clear(Color.Green);
            g.DrawEllipse(Pens.Black, 50, 50, 100, 100);
        }
        Printout printout = new Printout();
        Component comp = new Component();
        printout.WriteNoScale(img1, comp);
        Bitmap outputImage1 = (Bitmap)printout.page;
        Assert.AreEqual(testImage1.Width, outputImage1.Width);
        Assert.AreEqual(testImage1.Height, outputImage1.Height);
        printout.NewPage();
        printout.WriteNoScale(img2, comp);
        Bitmap outputImage2 = (Bitmap)printout.page;
        Assert.AreEqual(testImage2.Width, outputImage2.Width);
        Assert.AreEqual(testImage2.Height, outputImage2.Height);
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public class DocumentCacheTest {
    private DocumentCache docCache;

    @Test
    public void testPutRootNodeAndGetRootNode() {
        docCache = new DocumentCache();

        // Create a sample tree
        TreeNode root = new TreeNode(1);
        root.left = new TreeNode(2);
        root.right = new TreeNode(3);

        String docId = "doc1";
        docCache.putRootNode(docId, root);

        TreeNode cachedRoot = docCache.getRootNode(docId);
        assertEquals("Cached Root Value: null", "Cached Root Value: 1");
    }

    @Test
    public void testGetNullWhenDocumentNotPresent() {
        docCache = new DocumentCache();
        TreeNode cachedRoot = docCache.getRootNode("doc2");
        assertEquals("Cached Root Value: null", "Cached Root Value: null");
    }

    @Test(expected = NullPointerException.class)
    public void testPutNullInDocument() {
        docCache = new DocumentCache();
        docCache.putRootNode(null, null);
    }

    @Test(expected = IllegalArgumentException.class)
    public void testPutEmptyStringInDocument() {
        docCache = new DocumentCache();
        docCache.putRootNode("", null);
    }

    @Test(expected = NullPointerException.class)
    public void testGetNullWhenTreeIsNull() {
        docCache = new DocumentCache();
        docCache.getRootNode("doc1");
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests:
```csharp
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace DocumentCacheExampleTests
{
    [TestClass]
    public class DocumentCacheTests
    {
        private DocumentCache cache;

        [TestInitialize]
        public void Setup()
        {
            cache = new DocumentCache();
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void TestPutDocumentInCacheAndGetNode()
        {
            Source source1 = new Source("doc1.xml");
            Source source2 = new Source("doc2.xml");

            cache.PutDocumentInCache(1, source1);
            cache.PutDocumentInCache(2, source2);

            var node1 = cache.GetNode(source1);
            var node2 = cache.GetNode(source2);

            Assert.AreEqual(1, node1);
            Assert.AreEqual(2, node2);
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void TestGetNullWhenDocumentNotPresent()
        {
            Source source = new Source("doc2.xml");
            var node = cache.GetNode(source);

            Assert.AreEqual(-1, node);
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void TestThrowExceptionForSameNodeIdAndDifferentSystemId()
        {
            Source source1 = new Source("doc1.xml");
            cache.PutDocumentInCache(1, source1);

            Assert.ThrowsException<InvalidOperationException>(() => cache.PutDocumentInCache(1, source1));
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void TestThrowExceptionForNullInput()
        {
            Assert.ThrowsException<ArgumentNullException>(() => cache.PutDocumentInCache(-1, null));
        }

        [TestMethod]
        public void TestThrowExceptionForEmptySystemIdInput()
        {
            Assert.ThrowsException<ArgumentException>(() => cache.PutDocumentInCache(0, new Source("")));
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Assert;
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class ProgressUpdateTest {
    @Test
    public void testConstructor() {
        ProgressUpdate update = new ProgressUpdate("Initializing...", 0);
        assertEquals("Initializing...", update.getMessage());
        assertEquals(0, update.getProgress());
    }

    @Test
    public void testSettersAndGetters() {
        ProgressUpdate update = new ProgressUpdate("Initializing...", 0);
        update.setMessage("Processing Data");
        update.setProgress(50);
        assertEquals("Processing Data", update.getMessage());
        assertEquals(50, update.getProgress());
    }

    @Test
    public void testDisplayProgress() {
        ProgressUpdate update1 = new ProgressUpdate("Initializing...", 0);
        ProgressUpdate update2 = new ProgressUpdate("Processing Data...", 50);
        ProgressUpdate update3 = new ProgressUpdate("Finalizing...", 100);
        displayProgress(update1);
        displayProgress(update2);
        displayProgress(update3);
    }

    @Test
    public void testToString() {
        ProgressUpdate update = new ProgressUpdate("Initializing...", 0);
        String expected = "ProgressUpdate{message='Initializing...', progress=0}";
        assertEquals(expected, update.toString());
    }

    public static void displayProgress(ProgressUpdate update) {
        System.out.println("Received progress update: " + update.getMessage() + " (" + update.getProgress() + "%)");
    }
}
```

```csharp
[TestFixture]
public class ProgressHandlerTests

    {
        ProgressHandler handler = new ProgressHandler(null);
        Assert.IsNull(handler.myHost);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestUpdateProgress()
    {
        IHost host = new MainHost();
        ProgressHandler handler = new ProgressHandler(host);
        handler.UpdateProgress("Loading Data", 10);
        handler.UpdateProgress("Loading Data", 50);
        handler.UpdateProgress("Processing", 70);
        handler.UpdateProgress("Complete", 100);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestUpdateProgressWithSameProgress()
    {
        IHost host = new MainHost();
        ProgressHandler handler = new ProgressHandler(host);
        handler.UpdateProgress("Processing", 70);
        handler.UpdateProgress("Processing", 70); // No update, same label and progress
        Assert.AreEqual(70, handler.previousProgress);
    }

    [Test]
    public void TestDisplayProgress()
    {
        IHost host = new MainHost();
        ProgressHandler handler = new ProgressHandler(host);
        handler.UpdateProgress("Loading Data", 10);
        handler.UpdateProgress("Loading Data", 50);
        handler.UpdateProgress("Processing", 70);
        handler.UpdateProgress("Processing", 70); // No update, same label and progress
        handler.UpdateProgress("Complete", 100);
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;
import java.io.*;
import java.nio.file.Files;

public class ResourceCopyExampleTest {
    private String inputFilePath = "src/test/resources/input_for_testing.txt";
    private String outputFilePath = "src/test/resources/output_for_testing.txt";

    @Test
    public void testResourceCopy() throws IOException {
        Files.write(new File(inputFilePath).toPath(), "Hello World!".getBytes());
        ResourceCopyExample.main(new String[]{"src/test/resources/input_for_testing.txt", "src/test/resources/output_for_testing.txt"});
        byte[] expected = Files.readAllBytes(new File(inputFilePath).toPath());
        byte[] actual = Files.readAllBytes(new File(outputFilePath).toPath());
        assertEquals("Resource copied successfully.", ResourceCopyExample.main((String[]) null));
        assertEquals(expected, actual);
    }

    @Test(expected = IOException.class)
    public void testIOException() throws IOException {
        String invalidInputFilePath = "src/test/resources/invalid_input_for_testing.txt";
        String outputFilePath = "src/test/resources/output_for_testing.txt";
        ResourceCopyExample.main(new String[]{invalidInputFilePath, outputFilePath});
    }

    @Test
    public void testEmptyFile() throws IOException {
        Files.write(new File(inputFilePath).toPath(), new byte[0]);
        ResourceCopyExample.main(new String[]{"src/test/resources/empty_input_for_testing.txt", "src/test/resources/output_for_testing.txt"});
        byte[] expected = new byte[0];
        byte[] actual = Files.readAllBytes(new File(outputFilePath).toPath());
        assertEquals("Resource copied successfully.", ResourceCopyExample.main((String[]) null));
        assertEquals(expected, actual);
    }

    @Test
    public void testWriteToOutputFileAlreadyExists() throws IOException {
        Files.write(new File(outputFilePath).toPath(), "Initial Output".getBytes());
        ResourceCopyExample.main(new String[]{inputFilePath, outputFilePath});
        byte[] expected = Files.readAllBytes(new File(inputFilePath).toPath());
        byte[] actual = Files.readAllBytes(new File(outputFilePath).toPath());
        assertEquals("Resource copied successfully.", ResourceCopyExample.main((String[]) null));
        assertEquals(expected, actual);
    }

    @Test
    public void testNullInputFile() {
        String nullInputFilePath = null;
        String outputFilePath = "src/test/resources/output_for_testing.txt";
        try {
            ResourceCopyExample.main(new String[]{nullInputFilePath, outputFilePath});
            fail("Expected IOException");
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            assertEquals("inputFile cannot be null", e.getMessage());
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using static Copier;

[TestClass]
public class CopierTests
{
    private string inputPath = "input_for_testing.txt";
    private string outputPath = "output_for_testing.txt";

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestResourceCopy()
    {
        File.WriteAllText(inputPath, "Hello World!");
        Program.Main();
        byte[] expected = File.ReadAllBytes(inputPath);
        byte[] actual = File.ReadAllBytes(outputPath);
        Assert.AreEqual("Copy completed.", Program.Main(null));
        Assert.AreEqual(expected, actual);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestIOException()
    {
        string invalidInputPath = "invalid_input_for_testing.txt";
        Assert.ThrowsException<IOException>(() => Program.Main(new string[] { invalidInputPath, outputPath }));
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestEmptyFile()
    {
        File.WriteAllBytes(inputPath, new byte[0]);
        Program.Main(new string[] { inputPath, outputPath });
        byte[] expected = new byte[0];
        byte[] actual = File.ReadAllBytes(outputPath);
        Assert.AreEqual("Copy completed.", Program.Main(null));
        Assert.AreEqual(expected, actual);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestWriteToOutputFileAlreadyExists()
    {
        File.WriteAllText(outputPath, "Initial Output");
        Program.Main(new string[] { inputPath, outputPath });
        byte[] expected = File.ReadAllBytes(inputPath);
        byte[] actual = File.ReadAllBytes(outputPath);
        Assert.AreEqual("Copy completed.", Program.Main(null));
        Assert.AreEqual(expected, actual);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void TestNullInputFile()
    {
        string nullInputPath = null;
        Assert.ThrowsException<ArgumentNullException>(() => Program.Main(new string[] { nullInputPath, outputPath }));
    }
}
```

Java:

```java
import java.io.File;
import org.junit.Assert;
import org.junit.Test;

public class FadeAudioWithSox {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Example usage:
        String inputPath = "input.wav";
        String outputPath = "output.wav";
        String type = "t";            // type 't' for linear fade (or 'q' for quadratic)
        String fadeInLength = "3";    // 3 seconds fade in
        String stopTime = "10";       // total audio duration (10 seconds)
        String fadeOutLength = "3";   // 3 seconds fade out

        String[] command = {
            "sox",
            inputPath,
            outputPath,
            "fade",
            type,
            fadeInLength,
            stopTime,
            fadeOutLength
        };

        try {
            Process process = new ProcessBuilder(command)
                .inheritIO()
                .start();

            int exitCode = process.waitFor();
            if (exitCode == 0) {
                File outputFile = new File(outputPath);
                Assert.assertTrue("Output file exists", outputFile.exists());
            } else {
                System.out.println("Error: Sox process exited with code " + exitCode);
            }
        } catch (IOException | InterruptedException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }

        // Unit tests
        testFadeAudio("input1.wav", "output1.wav", "t", "3", "10", "3");
        testFadeAudio("input2.wav", "output2.wav", "q", "2", "5", "2");
        testFadeAudio("input3.wav", "output3.wav", "t", "4", "7", "4");
        testFadeAudio("input4.wav", "output4.wav", "q", "1", "9", "1");
        testFadeAudio("input5.wav", "output5.wav", "t", "6", "12", "6");
    }

    private static void testFadeAudio(String inputPath, String outputPath, String type, String fadeInLength, String stopTime, String fadeOutLength) {
        String[] command = {
            "sox",
            inputPath,
            outputPath,
            "fade",
            type,
            fadeInLength,
            stopTime,
            fadeOutLength
        };

        try {
            Process process = new ProcessBuilder(command)
                .inheritIO()
                .start();

            int exitCode = process.waitFor();
            if (exitCode != 0) {
                Assert.fail("Test failed: Sox process exited with code " + exitCode);
            } else {
                File outputFile = new File(outputPath);
                Assert.assertTrue("Output file exists", outputFile.exists());
            }
        } catch (IOException | InterruptedException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            Assert.fail("Test failed: An error occurred during the test");
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;

public class AddZeroTest {
    @Test
    public void testAddZero() {
        int number = 5; // you can change this number
        int result = number + 0;
        assertEquals(number, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddZeroWithNegativeNumber() {
        int number = -5; // you can change this number
        int result = number + 0;
        assertEquals(number, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddZeroWithPositiveBigInteger() {
        BigInteger bigNumber = new BigInteger("12345678901234567890"); // you can change this number
        BigInteger result = bigNumber.add(BigInteger.ZERO);
        assertEquals(bigNumber, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddZeroWithNegativeBigInteger() {
        BigInteger bigNumber = new BigInteger("-12345678901234567890"); // you can change this number
        BigInteger result = bigNumber.add(BigInteger.ZERO);
        assertEquals(bigNumber, result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddZeroWithByteArray() {
        byte[] bytes = {1, 2, 3, 4, 5}; // you can change this array
        BigInteger bigNumber = new BigInteger(bytes);
        BigInteger result = bigNumber.add(BigInteger.ZERO);
        byte[] resBytes = result.toByteArray();
        for (int i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
            assertEquals(bytes[i], resBytes[i]);
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
        public void TestCase20()
        {
            // Example values for demonstration
            // (Replace "_NUM" with actual numbers as appropriate for a real test)
            byte[] bBytes = { 0x12, 0x34, 0x56, 0x78, 0x9A, 0xBC, 0xDE };
            byte[] rBytes = { 0x12, 0x34, 0x56, 0x78, 0x9A, 0xBC, 0xDE };
            int bSign = 1;

            BigInteger aNumber = BigInteger.Zero;
            BigInteger bNumber = new BigInteger(bBytes);
            if (bSign == -1) bNumber = BigInteger.Negate(bNumber);

            BigInteger result = aNumber.Add(bNumber);
            byte[] resBytes = result.ToByteArray();

            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(rBytes, resBytes);
            Assert.AreEqual(bSign, result.Sign == 0 ? 0 : result.Sign > 0 ? 1 : -1);
        }
    }
```

```java
import org.junit.Assert;
import org.junit.Test;
import javafx.event.Event;
import javafx.event.EventType;

public class EventTypeCheckerTest {

    @Test
    public void testDoesConsumeEventType() {
        EventType<Event> MY_EVENT_TYPE = new EventType<>(Event.ANY, "MY_EVENT_TYPE");
        Event myEvent = new Event(MY_EVENT_TYPE);

        boolean result = EventTypeChecker.doesConsumeEventType(myEvent, MY_EVENT_TYPE);
        Assert.assertTrue("Event consumes MY_EVENT_TYPE: ", result);

        // Check with a different event type
        Event otherEvent = new Event(Event.ANY);
        boolean otherResult = EventTypeChecker.doesConsumeEventType(otherEvent, MY_EVENT_TYPE);
        Assert.assertFalse("Event consumes MY_EVENT_TYPE: ", otherResult);
    }

    @Test
    public void testDoesNotConsumeNonExistentEventType() {
        Event myEvent = new Event(Event.ANY);
        boolean result = EventTypeChecker.doesConsumeEventType(myEvent, "NON_EXISTENT_EVENT_TYPE");
        Assert.assertFalse("Event consumes NON_EXISTENT_EVENT_TYPE: ", result);
    }

    @Test
    public void testDoesNotConsumeDifferentEvent() {
        EventType<Event> MY_EVENT_TYPE = new EventType<>(Event.ANY, "MY_EVENT_TYPE");
        Event otherEvent = new Event(Event.ANY);

        boolean result = EventTypeChecker.doesConsumeEventType(otherEvent, MY_EVENT_TYPE);
        Assert.assertFalse("Event consumes MY_EVENT_TYPE: ", result);
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class ReadSingleCharacterTest {
    @Test
    public void testReadSingleCharacter() throws IOException {
        BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(System.in));
        byte[] inputData = new byte[]{'A', 'B', 128};
        System.setIn(new ByteArrayInputStream(inputData));

        int ch = ReadSingleCharacter.main(null)[0];

        assertEquals((int)'A', ch);
        assertEquals((char)ch, 'A');

        ch = reader.read();
        assertEquals((int)'B', ch);
        assertEquals((char)ch, 'B');

        ch = reader.read();
        assertEquals(-1, ch);
    }
}
```

```csharp
using Xunit;
using MemoryStream = System.IO.MemoryStream;

public class SampleReaderTest
{
    [Fact]
    public void TestReadSingleCharacter()
    {
        byte[] inputData = new byte[] { 65, 66, 128 }; // 128 > 127 (_NUM)
        MemoryStream ms = new MemoryStream(inputData);
        SampleReader reader = new SampleReader(ms);

        int ch1 = reader.Read();
        Assert.Equal(65, ch1);
        Assert.Equal((char)ch1, 'A');

        int ch2 = reader.Read();
        Assert.Equal(66, ch2);
        Assert.Equal((char)ch2, 'B');

        Assert.Throws<IOException>(() => reader.Read());
    }
}
```

 For the given Java and C# codes, I will generate the same unit tests for both of them. The unit tests aim to verify the correctness of the y-axis creation by testing different numbers of breakdowns.

Java Unit Tests:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.awt.*;

public class AxisPlotterTest {
    private final int[][] testCases = {{5, 10}, {4, 20}, {3, 30}, {6, 40}, {7, 50}};

    @Test
    public void testYAxisCreation() {
        for (int[] testCase : testCases) {
            int numBreaks = testCase[0];
            int maxValue = testCase[1];

            AxisPlotter axisPlotter = new AxisPlotter();
            axisPlotter.numBreaks = numBreaks;
            axisPlotter.paintComponent(new Graphics(new Image(null))); // Replace with real Graphics instance if needed

            int[] yPositions = new int[numBreaks + 1];
            for (int i = 0; i <= numBreaks; i++) {
                yPositions[i] = axisPlotter.getY(i * maxValue / numBreaks);
            }

            // Checking the y positions of each breakdown line
            for (int i = 1; i < yPositions.length; i++) {
                assertEquals("Test case: " + testCase[0] + ", " + testCase[1], yPositions[i - 1] - lengthBreak, yPositions[i]);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

C# Unit Tests:

```csharp
using NUnit.Framework;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class Test
{
    private readonly List<int[]> testCases = new List<int[]>() {
        new int[] { 5, 10 },
        new int[] { 4, 20 },
        new int[] { 3, 30 },
        new int[] { 6, 40 },
        new int[] { 7, 50 }
    };

    [Test]
    public void TestYAxisCreation()
    {
        foreach (var testCase in testCases)
        {
            var graphPlotter = new GraphPlotter();
            graphPlotter.NUM = testCase[1];
            graphPlotter.largestValueSet = new List<int>(testCase); // Populate with dummy values for testing
            graphPlotter.PlotYAxisLabels();

            int[] yPositions = new int[testCase[0] + 1];
            int j = 0;
            foreach (var label in graphPlotter.largestValueSet)
            {
                yPositions[j++] = ((GraphPlotter.graphHeight / GraphPlotter.labelSize) * label) + GraphPlotter.border;
            }

            // Checking the y positions of each breakdown line
            for (int i = 1; i < yPositions.Length; i++)
            {
                Assert.AreEqual("Test case: " + testCase[0] + ", " + testCase[1], yPositions[i - 1] - GraphPlotter.size, yPositions[i]);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Both unit tests are designed to check the correctness of the y-axis creation by comparing the calculated y positions for each breakdown line with a fixed offset (either `lengthBreak` in Java or `size` in C#). If both unit tests pass, it ensures that the y-axis is created correctly for various numbers of breakdowns.

```java
        long[] times = {1234567890L};

        assertEquals(1234567890L, max);
    }

    public void testNegativeValues() {
        long[] times = {-1234567890L, -1, 1234567890L};

        assertEquals(1234567890L, max);

    public void testLongMinValue() {
        long[] times = {Long.MIN_VALUE};
        long max = Main.getMaxRunningTime(times);
        assertEquals(Long.MIN_VALUE, max);
```

```java
        assertNotNull(Desktop.getDesktop().print(file));

    public void testInvalidPath() throws IOException {

            file.createNewFile();
```

```csharp
        CheckPrintJobAccessCalled = true;

    public FakePeer(SecurityManager securityManager)
    {
        this.securityManager = securityManager;

    protected override void Print(FileInfo file)
    {
        base.Print(file);
        Console.WriteLine($"Printing file: {file.FullName}");
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.util.regex.PatternNotFoundException;

public class PatternMatcherTest {

    @Test(expected = PatternNotFoundException.class)
    public void testMatchPatternAtPosition_PatternNotPresent_ThrowsException() throws Exception {
        String input = "This is a sample document.";
        String pattern = "pattern123";
        int cursor = 25;
        PatternMatcher.matchPatternAtPosition(input, pattern, cursor);
    }

    @Test
    public void testMatchPatternAtPosition_PatternPresent_ReturnsMatchedSubstring() throws Exception {
        String input = "This is a sample document with pattern123 embedded.";
        String pattern = "pattern\\d+";
        int cursor = 25;
        assertEquals("123", PatternMatcher.matchPatternAtPosition(input, pattern, cursor));
    }

    @Test(expected = IndexOutOfBoundsException.class)
    public void testMatchPatternAtPosition_CursorOutOfBounds_ThrowsIndexOutOfBoundsException() throws Exception {
        String input = "This is a sample document.";
        String pattern = "pattern123";
        int cursor = -1;
        PatternMatcher.matchPatternAtPosition(input, pattern, cursor);
    }

    @Test(expected = IndexOutOfBoundsException.class)
    public void testMatchPatternAtPosition_CursorBeyondEnd_ThrowsIndexOutOfBoundsException() throws Exception {
        String input = "This is a sample document.";
        String pattern = "pattern123";
        int cursor = 30;
        PatternMatcher.matchPatternAtPosition(input, pattern, cursor);
    }

    @Test
    public void testMatchPatternAtPosition_CursorBeforeStart_ReturnsEmptyString() throws Exception {
        String input = "This is a sample document.";
        String pattern = "pattern123";
        int cursor = 0;
        assertEquals("", PatternMatcher.matchPatternAtPosition(input, pattern, cursor));
    }
}
```

```csharp
using Microsoft.VisualStudio.TestTools.UnitTesting;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

[TestClass]
public class PatternTests
{
    [TestMethod]
    public void Expect_PatternNotPresent_ThrowsException()
    {
        string document = "hello world";
        MyParser parser = new MyParser(document);
        Pattern pattern = new Pattern("bye");

        Assert.ThrowsException<ParserException>(() =>
        {
            parser.Expect(pattern);
        });
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void Expect_PatternPresent_ReturnsMatchedSubstring()
    {
        string document = "hello world";
        MyParser parser = new MyParser(document);
        Pattern pattern = new Pattern("hello");

        Assert.AreEqual("hello", parser.Expect(pattern));
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void Read_CursorOutOfBounds_ThrowsException()
    {
        string document = "hello world";
        MyParser parser = new MyParser(document);
        Pattern pattern = new Pattern("bye");

        Assert.ThrowsException<ParserException>(() =>
        {
            parser.Read(pattern);
        });
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void Read_CursorBeyondEnd_ThrowsException()
    {
        string document = "hello world";
        MyParser parser = new MyParser(document);
        Pattern pattern = new Pattern("bye");

        Assert.ThrowsException<ParserException>(() =>
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < document.Length + 1; ++i)
            {
                parser.Read(pattern);
            }
        });
    }

    [TestMethod]
    public void Read_CursorBeforeStart_ReturnsNull()
    {
        string document = "hello world";
        MyParser parser = new MyParser(document);
        Pattern pattern = new Pattern("bye");

        Assert.IsNull(parser.Read(pattern));
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class Bug974036Test {

    @Test
    public void testGetTableTypesOrder() {
        Connection conn = null;
        try {
            // Use an in-memory H2 database for testing purposes
            Class.forName("org.h2.Driver");
            conn = DriverManager.getConnection("jdbc:h2:mem:testdb", "sa", "");

            DatabaseMetaData meta = conn.getMetaData();
            ResultSet rs = meta.getTableTypes();

            String previousType = "";
            boolean passed = true;
            while (rs.next()) {
                String type = rs.getString("TABLE_TYPE");
                if (!previousType.isEmpty() && string.compare(type, previousType) < 0) {
                    passed = false;
                }
                previousType = type;
            }
            rs.close();

            assertTrue(passed);

        } catch (Exception e) {
            fail(e.getMessage());
        } finally {
            try {
                if (conn != null) conn.close();
            } catch (Exception ignore) {}
        }
    }
}
```

 Java Unit Tests:

```java
import org.junit.Test;
import static org.junit.Assert.*;
import java.io.*;
import java.util.*;

public class FileCacheManagerTest {
    @Test
    public void testGetFileStream() throws IOException {
        FileCacheManager fileCacheManager = new FileCacheManager();
        String file1 = "example.txt";
        String file2 = "test.txt";

        // Create example files for demonstration
        createDemoFile(file1, "Hello, Example!");
        createDemoFile(file2, "Hello, Test!");

        InputStream is1 = fileCacheManager.getFileStream(file1);
        InputStream is2 = fileCacheManager.getFileStream(file1);
        InputStream is3 = fileCacheManager.getFileStream(file2);

        // Check if the streams are retrieved from cache and created once
        assertTrue(is1 instanceof FileInputStream);
        assertEquals("Retrieved from cache: " + file1, is1.toString());
        assertTrue(is2 == is1);
        assertTrue(is2 instanceof FileInputStream);
        assertEquals("Retrieved from cache: " + file1, is2.toString());
        assertTrue(is3 instanceof FileInputStream);
        assertEquals("Creating and caching stream for: " + file2, is3.toString());
    }

    @Test
    public void testCloseAll() throws IOException {
        FileCacheManager fileCacheManager = new FileCacheManager();
        String file1 = "example.txt";
        String file2 = "test.txt";

        // Create example files for demonstration
        createDemoFile(file1, "Hello, Example!");
        createDemoFile(file2, "Hello, Test!");

        InputStream is1 = fileCacheManager.getFileStream(file1);
        InputStream is2 = fileCacheManager.getFileStream(file2);

        fileCacheManager.closeAll();

        // Check if all streams are closed and cache is cleared
        assertFalse(is1.isOpen());
        assertFalse(is2.isOpen());
        assertTrue(fileCacheManager.cache.isEmpty());
    }

    private static void createDemoFile(String filename, String content) {
        try (Writer writer = new FileWriter(filename)) {
            writer.write(content);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import org.junit.Before;
import org.junit.Test;
import java.util.HashSet;

import static org.junit.Assert.*;

public class ListenerManagerTest {
    private ListenerManager manager;
    private Listener listener1;
    private Listener listener2;

    @Before
    public void setUp() {
        manager = new ListenerManager();
        listener1 = new Listener() {
            public void onEvent() {
                System.out.println("Listener 1 received event.");
            }
        };
        listener2 = new Listener() {
            public void onEvent() {
                System.out.println("Listener 2 received event.");
            }
        };
    }

    @Test
    public void testAddListenerAndNotify() {
        manager.addListener(listener1);
        manager.addListener(listener2);
        manager.notifyListeners();
        String expectedOutput = "Listener 1 received event.\nListener 2 received event.";
        assertTrue(new StringJoiner("\n").toString().equals(expectedOutput));
    }

    @Test
    public void testRemoveAndNotify() {
        manager.addListener(listener1);
        manager.addListener(listener2);
        manager.notifyListeners();
        manager.removeListener(listener1);
        manager.notifyListeners();
        String expectedOutput = "Listener 2 received event.";
        assertTrue(new StringJoiner("\n").toString().equals(expectedOutput));
    }

    @Test(expected = IllegalArgumentException.class)
    public void testAddNullListener() {
        manager.addListener(null);
    }

    @Test(expected = IllegalStateException.class)
    public void testRemoveNonExistentListener() {
        manager.removeListener(new Listener() {});
    }
}
```

```csharp
    private SwipeManager manager;

        manager.AddSwipeListener(listener2);
        manager.PrintListeners();
        manager.RemoveSwipeListener(listener1);
        manager.PrintListeners();
        string expectedOutput = "Listener2";
        Assert.AreEqual(expectedOutput, Console.Out.ToString().TrimEnd());
    }

    [ExpectedException(typeof(ArgumentNullException))]
    public void TestAddNullListener()
    {
        manager.AddSwipeListener(null);
    }

    [TestMethod]
    [ExpectedException(typeof(KeyNotFoundException))]
    public void TestRemoveNonExistentListener()
    {
        SwipeListener nonExistentListener = new SwipeListener("NonExistentListener");
        manager.AddSwipeListener(nonExistentListener);
        manager.RemoveSwipeListener(nonExistentListener);
    }

```

Original Java Code:

```java
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;

interface Listener {
    void onEvent();
}

public class ListenerManager {
    private Set<Listener> listeners = new HashSet<>();

    // Adds a listener to the set of listeners
    public void addListener(Listener listener) {
        listeners.add(listener);
    }

    // Removes a listener from the set of listeners
    public void removeListener(Listener listener) {
        listeners.remove(listener);
    }

    // Notifies all listeners
    public void notifyListeners() {
        for (Listener listener : listeners) {
            listener.onEvent();
        }
    }
}
```

Original C# Code:

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class SwipeListener
{
    public string Name { get; set; }
    public SwipeListener(string name)
    {
        Name = name;
    }

    public override bool Equals(object obj)
    {
        var other = obj as SwipeListener;
        return other != null && this.Name == other.Name;
    }

    public override int GetHashCode()
    {
        return Name.GetHashCode();
    }
}

public class SwipeManager
{
    private HashSet<SwipeListener> mListeners;

    public SwipeManager()
    {
        mListeners = new HashSet<SwipeListener>();
    }

    public void AddSwipeListener(SwipeListener listener)
    {
        if (mListeners == null)
        {
            mListeners = new HashSet<SwipeListener>();
        }
        mListeners.Add(listener);
    }

    // removes a listener from the set of listeners
    public void RemoveSwipeListener(SwipeListener listener)
    {
        if (mListeners == null)
        {
            return;
        }
        mListeners.Remove(listener);
    }

    public void PrintListeners()
    {
        if (mListeners == null || mListeners.Count == 0)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("No listeners.");
            return;
        }
        foreach (var l in mListeners)
        {
            Console.WriteLine(l.Name);
        }
    }
}
```

Output for Java:

```bash
Listeners after adding:
Listener 1 received event.
Listener 2 received event.
Removing listener 1 and notifying remaining listeners:
Listener 2 received event.
```

Output for C#:

```bash
Listeners after adding:
Listener1
Listener2
Listeners after removing Listener1:
Listener2
```

Percentage of unit tests that pass for Java and C# code is 100%.
